### [1] [FailSAE: Towards Interpretable Failure Prediction for Vision-Language Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04276)
*Jie Ma,Zongxi Liu,Yi Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）对视觉-语言模型（VLM）进行可解释的失败预测，通过三阶段失败感知训练管道，使SAE潜在方向保持可解释性并提升失败预测能力，实验表明该方法优于基线，并支持概念级分析和运行时失败恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在关键领域应用越来越广泛，失败预测对风险感知部署和人工干预至关重要。现有方法（如置信度或辅助分类器）虽能预测失败，但可解释性有限，难以理解失败原因，因此需要更可解释的失败预测方法。

Method: 将失败预测形式化为对稀疏SAE潜在激活的分类任务，并设计三阶段失败感知训练管道：在保持SAE潜在方向可解释性的同时，增强其用于失败预测的信息量。通过该管道学习到的潜在方向用于分类，并进一步用于概念级分析和运行时恢复。

Result: 实验表明所提框架在失败预测上优于评估的基线方法。进一步分析显示，失败感知训练促使SAE潜在方向捕获更多类别特定概念。概念级分析揭示失败期间模型表征从类别特定概念转向模糊或风格相关概念。此外，学习到的SAE潜在方向可支持运行时失败恢复。

Conclusion: SAE结合失败感知训练能提供可解释且有效的VLM失败预测，并支持概念级理解与运行时恢复，为高可信度VLM部署提供了新途径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs), such as CLIP, have achieved strong performance across multimodal tasks by aligning visual and textual representations in a shared embedding space. As VLMs are increasingly used for high-stakes domains, failure prediction becomes critical for risk-aware deployment and human intervention. Existing failure prediction methods typically rely on confidence scores or auxiliary classifiers. Although these methods are effective on predicting VLM failures, they provide limited interpretability. In this work, we investigate the use of Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) for interpretable failure prediction in VLMs. We formulate failure prediction as a classification task over sparse SAE latent activations and introduce a three-stage failure-aware training pipeline that encourages the learned latent directions to remain interpretable while becoming more informative for failure prediction. Our experiments show that the resulting framework outperforms the evaluated baselines in failure prediction. Further analysis suggests that failure-aware training encourages SAE latent directions to capture more class-specific concepts. We also use the SAE to provide a concept-level analysis of how model representations change during failures, revealing a shift from class-specific concepts toward more ambiguous or style-related concepts. Finally, we explore how the learned SAE latent directions can support runtime failure recovery.

</details>


### [2] [When Seeing Overrides Knowing: Visual Dominance and Deferral-Based Method for Personalized Safety in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04281)
*Edward Sun,Yuchen Wu,Zixian Ma,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Yijia Xiao,Xiaoyuan Yi,Ranjay Krishna,Wei Wang,Jindong Wang,Aylin Caliskan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态视觉语言模型（VLM）中的个性化安全问题，提出了MPS-Bench基准（涵盖12个高风险领域的584张真实图像、5,181个场景，每个场景配有一个隐藏用户画像）。通过评估8个前沿VLM，发现模型在86-99%的情况下直接回答而非寻求缺失上下文，且个性化安全得分均不超过2.6/5。通过因果干预分析，作者揭示了"视觉主导"的两阶段机制：视觉信息在早期层进入文本表征并抑制文本风险信号，导致晚期内部修复不可靠。基于此机制提出轻量级输入监控器PRISM，利用双向跨模态调制预测需要延迟/转介的查询，在全部测试模型上达到0.978的AUC，并严格占据安全-效用帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM正被部署在高风险场景中，一个一般性合理的回答对特定用户（其医疗、情绪或情境上下文对模型未知）而言可能不安全。现有研究缺乏对多模态系统中"个性化安全"问题的系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 构建MPS-Bench基准：从584张真实图像生成5,181个场景，覆盖12个高风险领域，每个场景配对隐藏用户画像；2) 评估8个前沿VLM的个性化安全表现；3) 通过多模态交互分析与因果干预揭示失败机制，识别视觉主导的两阶段机制；4) 提出PRISM轻量级输入监控器，采用双向跨模态调制预测查询是否需要延迟/转介。

Result: 所有测试VLM在86-99%的情况下直接作答而非寻求上下文；个性化安全分数最高仅2.6/5。因果分析证实视觉信息在早期层进入文本流、抑制文本风险信号，并经修改后的文本表征决定最终决策，使晚期干预失效。PRISM实现0.978 AUC，在全部模型上严格支配安全-效用帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 个性化安全在多模态VLM中是一个严重且未被解决的关键问题，其根源在于多模态融合中的视觉主导机制。PRISM表明，通过输入端的轻量级监控（而非事后内部干预）可在不牺牲效用的前提下显著提升个性化安全性，为高可靠多模态系统提供了一条可行的工程路径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, where a response that is reasonable in general may still be unsafe for a particular user whose medical, emotional, or situational context is unknown to the model. We study this problem of personalized safety in multimodal systems and introduce MPS-Bench, a benchmark of 5,181 scenarios from 584 real-world images across 12 high-risk domains, each paired with a hidden user profile. Evaluating eight frontier VLMs, we find that they almost always respond directly (86-99%) rather than seek missing context, and none exceeds 2.6/5 on personalized safety. To understand why these failures arise, we analyze multimodal interactions and identify visual dominance: visual information enters text representations early and suppresses textual risk signals during multimodal fusion. Causal interventions reveal a two-stage mechanism in which visual affect is first transferred into the text stream in early layers and then shapes the final decision through this altered text representation, making late-stage internal remediation unreliable. Motivated by this mechanism, we propose PRISM, a lightweight input monitor that uses bidirectional cross-modal modulation to predict when a query is likely to require deferral. PRISM achieves 0.978 AUC and strictly dominates the safety-utility Pareto frontier across all tested models.

</details>


### [3] [Step Back to Move Forward: Reflection-Aware Preference Optimization for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04282)
*Junlong Wu,Jiuzhou Lin,Jia Sun,Boheng Zhang,Huaiqing Wang,Dewen Fan,Houde Liu,Qianqian Gan,Fan Yang,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RA-GRPO框架，通过将"反向反思"纳入扩散模型的强化学习偏好对齐过程。核心包含两项技术：扩散反思（Diffusion Reflection）利用弱估计器反转扩散过程以修正中间采样轨迹，引导潜状态朝向真实数据流形高概率区域；反事实路径合成（Counterfactual Path Synthesis）隐式蒸馏修正轨迹到策略中，使模型内化基于搜索的探索收益而无需推理开销。实验表明该方法在T2I和T2V任务上显著优于现有方法，尤其在缓解奖励黑客和提升泛化方面表现突出，且架构无关、可无缝集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RL作为扩散生成模型的后训练策略展现出强大潜力，但现有的基于策略梯度的方法探索效率低下，容易陷入局部最优，从而损害生成结果的语义忠实度和视觉真实性。作者旨在通过引入"反向反思"机制改进"前向"生成，解决探索效率不足与奖励黑客等问题。

Method: 提出RA-GRPO框架，包含两大核心组件：(1) 扩散反思——通过弱估计器反转扩散过程来修正中间采样轨迹，将潜状态引导至真实数据流形的高概率区域；(2) 反事实路径合成——隐式地将修正后的轨迹蒸馏进策略中，使模型内化搜索式探索的益处而不增加推理开销。整体作为强化学习偏好对齐的新框架，架构无关且可无缝集成到标准流程。

Result: 在文本到图像（T2I）和文本到视频（T2V）模型上的广泛实验表明，RA-GRPO显著优于现有方法，特别在缓解奖励黑客现象和提升泛化能力方面效果突出。

Conclusion: RA-GRPO为扩散生成模型的稳定偏好对齐提供了有前景的方向，通过结合反向反思与反事实路径合成，有效提升了探索效率和生成质量，同时保持架构通用性和集成便利性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the mainstream paradigm for modern visual generation and have substantially advanced multimedia content synthesis, especially in text-to-image and text-to-video tasks. To further align such generative models with human preferences, reinforcement learning (RL) has recently shown strong potential as a post-training strategy. Nevertheless, existing policy gradient-based methods often explore inefficiently, making them vulnerable to local optima that may degrade semantic faithfulness and visual realism. To address these challenges, we present Reflection-Aware GRPO (RA-GRPO), a new RL-based preference alignment framework for diffusion generative models. The core idea is to improve "forward" generation by incorporating "backward" reflection during optimization. We first introduce Diffusion Reflection, which rectifies intermediate sampling trajectories by inverting the diffusion process with a weak estimator, guiding latent states toward higher-probability regions of the true data manifold. Furthermore, we introduce Counterfactual Path Synthesis to implicitly distill these rectified trajectories into the policy, enabling the model to internalize the benefits of search-based exploration without incurring inference-time overhead. Extensive experiments on T2I and T2V models demonstrate that RA-GRPO significantly outperforms existing methods, particularly in mitigating reward hacking and improving generalization. The method remains architecture-agnostic and integrates seamlessly with standard pipelines, suggesting a promising direction for stable preference alignment.

</details>


### [4] [Joint Alignment and Distillation for Video Generation via Sample-Guided Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04283)
*Jiuzhou Lin,Junlong Wu,Fei Zuo,Huan Ouyang,Dewen Fan,Boheng Zhang,Huaiqing Wang,Jia Sun,Fan Yang,Houde Liu,Kehai Chen,Min Zhang,Tingting Gao,Han Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DM-Align，一种基于分布匹配（DM）的统一单阶段优化框架，用于视频生成模型的人类偏好对齐。该方法将传统分离的 RL 与蒸馏阶段合并，通过推导互补的梯度方向引导模型朝向人类偏好样本，消除了多步奖励评估和复杂 ODE-SDE 转换，同时提升了蒸馏质量与偏好对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型与人类偏好的对齐高度依赖强化学习（RL），但 RL 存在巨大的计算开销。现有工作流通常将 RL 与蒸馏视为两个割裂的阶段：先 RL 后蒸馏会带来高昂计算成本，而先蒸馏后 RL 则常导致模型坍塌。作者旨在克服这一两难困境，提出统一的单阶段优化框架。

Method: 基于标准分布匹配（DM）框架，即蒸馏通过最小化真实模型与伪造模型之间的差距来更新模型（指引生成朝向清晰与高保真）。在此基础上，DM-Align 推导出一个互补的梯度方向来引导模型朝向人类偏好的样本。受 DPO 和 GRPO 启发，该方法利用分布差异——由偏好对或组内探索构建——直接构造该偏好引导梯度。通过协同这两个梯度方向，消除了传统 RL 中多步奖励评估与复杂 ODE-SDE 转换的需求。

Result: 在多个基础视频模型上的综合实验表明，该样本引导框架能够稳健地增强蒸馏质量与偏好对齐，且一致性地优于独立变体以及顺序两阶段流水线。

Conclusion: DM-Align 通过统一单阶段分布匹配框架成功将 RL 偏好对齐与知识蒸馏融合，既避免了先 RL 后蒸馏的高昂成本，也规避了先蒸馏后 RL 的模型坍塌问题，为视频生成模型的对齐提供了高效且稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Aligning video generative models to human preferences heavily relies on Reinforcement Learning (RL), which suffers from extensive computational overhead. Existing workflows typically treat RL and distillation as disconnected stages: applying RL before distillation incurs prohibitive computational costs, whereas applying RL after distillation frequently leads to model collapse. To overcome these limitations, we propose a unified, single-stage optimization framework grounded in Distribution Matching (DM). In the standard DM framework, distillation updates the model via a gradient direction that minimizes the gap between the real and fake models, guiding generations toward clarity and high fidelity. Building upon this, we introduce DM-Align, which derives a complementary gradient direction to guide the model toward human-preferred samples. Inspired by DPO and GRPO, our method leverages the distributional gap -- formulated from either preference pairs or intra-group exploration -- to directly construct this preference-guided gradient. By synergizing these two gradient directions, our approach eliminates the need for multi-step reward evaluation and complex ODE-SDE conversions inherent in traditional RL. Comprehensive experiments across multiple foundational video models demonstrate that this sample-guided framework robustly enhances both distillation quality and preference alignment, consistently outperforming both standalone variants and sequential two-stage pipelines.

</details>


### [5] [The microscope is the mask: privileged views and labels from a cryo-ET forward model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04325)
*Bogdan Toader,Kiarash Jamali,Tanmay A. M. Bharat,Sjors H. W. Scheres*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CARNIVAL模型，利用模拟数据训练用于拥挤冷冻电子断层扫描（cryo-ET）体积中的蛋白质注释任务。其核心创新在于：1）利用前向模型引入的伪影生成同一场景的域特定增强配对视图，用于LeJEPA自监督框架中的不变性目标；2）利用模拟管线中的蛋白质位置和身份等特权信息来指导模型架构和损失函数，使语义信息局部化于蛋白质位置。模型无需微调即可在真实断层扫描数据集的分类与检测任务中优于使用对比目标训练的SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限倾斜角度采集且被测量算子严重污染的冷冻电子断层扫描重建体积中，蛋白质注释面临巨大挑战。现有模拟数据训练方法未充分考虑前向模型造成的域差距，且未利用模拟数据中可获得的特权信息来提升语义定位能力。

Method: 1) 利用前向模型的伪影生成同场景的域特定增强配对视图，在LeJEPA自监督框架中引入不变性目标；2) 使用模拟管线中的蛋白质位置和身份等额外信息指导模型架构与损失设计，使语义信息在稠密特征体积中定位于蛋白质位置；3) 在真实断层扫描上直接评估（无微调），涵盖多种蛋白质类型和两种断层扫描处理类型。

Result: CARNIVAL在真实断层扫描数据的分类和检测任务中优于采用对比目标训练但未使用前向模型配对视图或特权信息的SOTA模型。

Conclusion: 前向模型感知的数据增强与模拟特权信息的利用可显著提升模拟到真实迁移下的蛋白质注释性能，为crowded cryo-ET场景提供有效训练范式。

Abstract: We explore the use of simulated data for training a model for protein annotation in crowded cryo-electron tomography volumes reconstructed from images collected at limited tilt angles and severely corrupted by the measurement operator. Firstly, we leverage the corruptions imposed by the forward model to generate domain-specific augmented paired views of the exact same scene for an invariance objective integrated into the LeJEPA self-supervised training framework. Secondly, we use additional information from the simulation pipeline such as the positions and identity of proteins in the simulated volumes to inform the architecture of the model and the loss function, so that semantic information is localised at protein positions in the resulting dense feature volume. The resulting model, CARNIVAL, is evaluated without finetuning on classification and detection tasks in real tomograms, using a benchmark dataset containing multiple protein types and two tomogram processing types. We show that CARNIVAL outperforms a state-of-the-art model trained using a contrastive objective on simulated data but without forward model-based paired views or privileged information.

</details>


### [6] [Object Concepts Emerge from Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04348)
*Boshi Li,Xiaohui Wang,Xiaoyang Wu,Zhichao Li,Ya Yang,Naiyan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受生物学启发的框架，利用运动边界（off-the-shelf光流与聚类生成伪实例掩码）为单图像编码器提供像素级成对度量学习监督，从而从原始视频中学习目标中心化表征。无需人工标注或相机标定，通过Motion-Verified自训练将监督从1.95亿帧扩展到4.21亿帧，训练至Swin-H并蒸馏到Swin骨干网络。在单目深度估计、3D目标检测、3D占用预测和端到端规划等任务上达到或超越有监督/自监督预训练基线，尤其在几何和实例敏感任务上迁移效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉预训练方法往往只捕获语义类别，而未能保持单个实例的身份与连贯性。目标中心化的视觉表征对物理世界感知至关重要，因此需要探索一种不依赖人工标注和相机标定、能从原始视频中学习实例级表征的预训练方法。

Method: 1) 以运动边界作为目标级分组的监督来源：利用现成光流和聚类生成伪实例掩码；2) 用这些掩码对单图像编码器施加像素级成对度量学习；3) 首先从7,163小时驾驶与网络视频获得1.95亿伪标注帧，随后通过Motion-Verified自训练（结合模型建议与运动证据）将监督扩展到4.21亿帧；4) 训练至Swin-H并在Swin骨干家族上进行蒸馏。

Result: 在单目深度估计、3D目标检测、3D占用预测和端到端规划等下游任务上，模型取得与有监督和自监督预训练基线相当或更优的性能，尤其在几何和实例敏感任务上迁移表现突出。

Conclusion: 运动衍生的监督能够教会静态图像编码器表征视觉实例，为可扩展的视觉预训练提供了一条互补方向。

Abstract: Object-centric visual representations are important for physical-world perception, but existing visual pretraining methods often capture semantic categories without preserving the identity and coherence of individual instances. We present a biologically inspired framework that learns object-centric representations for single images from raw videos. Our approach uses motion boundaries as a source of object-level grouping: off-the-shelf optical flow and clustering produce pseudo-instance masks, which supervise a single-image encoder with pixel-level pairwise metric learning. The framework requires neither human annotations nor camera calibration. We first obtain 195 million pseudo-labeled frames from 7,163 hours of driving and web videos, then expand the supervision to 421 million frames with Motion-Verified Self-Training, which combines model proposals with motion evidence. We train encoders up to Swin-H and distill the learned representations into a family of Swin backbones. Across monocular depth estimation, 3D object detection, 3D occupancy prediction, and end-to-end planning, the resulting models achieve competitive or superior performance relative to supervised and self-supervised pretraining baselines, with particularly strong transfer on geometry- and instance-sensitive tasks. These results show that motion-derived supervision can teach static image encoders to represent visual instances, providing a complementary direction for scalable visual pretraining.

</details>


### [7] [AdaptVPR: Route-Aware Hard Positive Generation for Robust Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04369)
*Shunpeng Chen,Jingyi Zhang,Changwei Wang,Shengpeng Xu,Yukun Song,Xingtian Pei,Jinzhou Lin,Li Guo,Shibiao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptVPR，一个基于路线感知的生成式增强框架，用于为视觉地点识别（VPR）网络构建同地点的困难正样本，以缓解域偏移导致的鲁棒性退化问题。该框架利用视觉语言模型解析场景属性并估计编辑可行性，通过规则调度器根据可编辑性分数和风险约束决定生成路线，将生成过程分解为全局外观路线、局部遮挡路线和双路线三种互补类型，并用基于几何一致性和外观多样性的VPR验证方案过滤生成候选，最终构建了含16万验证通过的同地点困难正样本的AdaptCities数据集，在多个VPR基线上带来最高9.2%的R@1提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VPR的鲁棒性常因光照、天气、季节变化和动态遮挡等域偏移而退化，其中一项关键原因是现有训练数据中同一地点的外观多样性有限，难以让网络学到跨域不变的表示。

Method: AdaptVPR首先使用视觉语言模型（VLM）解析场景属性并评估编辑可行性，同时由基于规则的调度器根据可编辑性分数和风险约束确定生成路线。生成过程分为三条互补路线：全局外观路线（天气、光照、时间的全局变化）、局部遮挡路线（插入合理动态遮挡物）和双路线（两者组合，产生更具挑战性的外观偏移）。每个生成候选通过基于几何一致性和外观多样性的VPR定向验证方案评估，降低结构漂移风险同时保证外观变化。全局候选一次性生成，验证失败即拒绝；局部遮挡和双路线候选利用验证反馈进行有限提示的精炼和再生成。

Result: 利用该框架构建了AdaptCities数据集，包含16万个经过验证的合成同地点困难正样本。在多个VPR基线和视觉基础骨干网络上的实验表明，在标准基准上有一致性能提升，在具有挑战性的域偏移场景下提升显著，R@1最多提升9.2%。源码和数据公开于GitHub。

Conclusion: AdaptVPR验证了路线感知的生成式增强能有效扩充同地点外观多样性并构造困难正样本，从而提升VPR的跨域鲁棒性；验证反馈机制与风险约束能在保证生成真实性的同时避免结构漂移。

Abstract: Visual Place Recognition (VPR) localizes a query image by retrieving database images of the same or nearby place, yet its robustness is often degraded by domain shifts arising from illumination, weather, seasonal changes, and dynamic occlusions. One contributing factor is the limited appearance diversity of the same place in existing training data. To address this issue, we propose AdaptVPR, a route-aware generative augmentation framework that constructs same-place hard positives for robust VPR training. AdaptVPR first uses a vision language model to parse scene attributes and estimate editing feasibility, while a rule-based scheduler determines the generation route according to editability scores and risk constraints. The generation process is decomposed into three complementary routes: the Global Appearance Route introduces global scene changes in weather, illumination, and time of day; the Local Occlusion Route inserts plausible dynamic occluders; and the Dual Route combines both types of perturbations to produce more challenging appearance shifts. Each generated candidate is evaluated using a VPR-oriented verification scheme based on geometric consistency and appearance diversity, reducing the risk of structural drift while ensuring sufficient appearance variation. Global candidates are generated once and rejected if verification fails, while Local Occlusion and Dual candidates use verification feedback for limited prompt refinement and regeneration. Using this framework, we construct AdaptCities, containing 160K verified synthetic same-place hard positives. Experiments across multiple VPR baselines and vision foundation backbones show consistent gains on standard benchmarks and substantial improvements under challenging domain shifts, with R@1 gains of up to 9.2%. The source code and data resources are publicly available at https://github.com/chenshunpeng/AdaptVPR.

</details>


### [8] [Where Appearance Fails, Geometry Recognizes: A CAD-Free 3D Shape Prior That Complements Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04381)
*Chenxi Tao,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究在没有标注训练集且缺少CAD模型的情况下，如何利用短时物体中心扫描来提升特定物体识别性能。方法是用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）重建物体、聚合成逐类形状原型，并与冻结的DINOv2图像特征融合。实验表明：几何信息（来自RGB-D深度、3DGS、CAD）能达到相近的识别性能，3DGS仅是获取点云的便捷途径；收益取决于物体形状的可辨识度——形状独特的物体增益显著，形状易混淆的低纹理工业件增益较小但稳定；几何先验是互补的，能修复更多图像失败而非破坏成功，且在部分遮挡下收益增长；价值在于几何本身而非渲染像素，识别几乎对光照不变。研究限定于识别任务，不涉及BOP位姿基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在制造与服务机器人场景中，常需在无标签训练集的情况下识别已上线的特定物体。传统可渲染先验（CAD模型）往往不可用；纯二维图像缺乏形状先验，冻结的底层视觉特征在几何相似、低纹理的工业零件上表现不佳。作者想探究：一次短时物体中心扫描相比单纯图像，能为识别带来多少额外价值，以及价值来源是什么。

Method: 对每个物体进行一次短时物体中心扫描，用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）重建物体，再将重建结果聚合成逐类形状原型，最后与冻结的DINOv2图像特征融合进行识别。研究通过控制变量对比几何来源（RGB-D深度、3DGS、CAD）的效果，并考察融合权重、遮挡、光照等影响因素。

Result: 1) RGB-D深度、3DGS与CAD三种几何来源的识别性能相当（HOPE持平，T-LESS相差不超过1.6点），说明3DGS只是获取点云的便捷途径。2) 收益由形状可辨识度决定：形状独特的HOPE上，纯几何达0.920（图像仅0.832），固定权重融合为0.872；形状易混淆的低纹理T-LESS上，融合从0.560提升到0.591，增益小但稳定且高于任一单一信号。3) 先验是互补而非均匀加性的：修复的图像失败远多于破坏的成功，且部分遮挡下收益增长。4) 价值在几何而非渲染像素：3DGS渲染对图像侧无帮助，冻结特征识别几乎光照不变（波动在2.5点以内）。

Conclusion: 短时扫描重建提供的几何形状先验能有效替代缺失的CAD信息以提升无标签物体识别性能，且该价值源于几何信息本身而非渲染图像。先验是互补的，尤其有助于挽救图像识别失败并在遮挡下更有效。研究限定于识别任务而非BOP位姿基准，指出基于冻结DINOv2特征的识别具有近光照不变性，可在工业场景中实用部署。

Abstract: Recognizing specific objects onboarded without a labeled training set recurs across manufacturing and service robotics, yet the conventional renderable prior, a computer-aided-design (CAD) model, is often unavailable. Two-dimensional capture supplies no shape prior, and frozen foundation features fail on geometrically similar, low-texture industrial parts. We ask what a short object-centric scan buys for recognition beyond the captured images themselves: each object is reconstructed with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), summarized into a per-class shape prototype, and fused with frozen DINOv2 image features. First, the scan recovers the recognition value of CAD without CAD: geometry from RGB-D depth (on T-LESS), 3DGS, and CAD gives comparable recognition (tied on HOPE, within 1.6 points on T-LESS); 3DGS is only a convenient route to a point cloud. Second, the payoff is governed by how recognizable the shape is: on shape-distinctive household objects (HOPE) geometry alone reaches 0.920 versus image-only 0.832, a ceiling below which fixed-weight fusion (0.872) sits. On shape-confusable textureless industrial parts (T-LESS) the gain is modest but consistent (0.560 to 0.591 fused, above both single signals). Third, the prior is complementary, not uniformly additive: it rescues far more image failures than it breaks successes, and its benefit grows under partial occlusion. Finally, the worth lies in geometry, not rendered pixels: 3DGS renderings do not help the image side, and frozen-feature recognition is nearly lighting-invariant (within 2.5 points). The study is scoped to recognition, not the BOP pose benchmark.

</details>


### [9] [What Moves? Localized Motion Representations for Compositional Scene Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04383)
*Frank Fundel,Malek Ben Alaya,Thomas Ressler-Antal,Stefan Andreas Baumann,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可提示的局部运动表征方法，为空间掩码指定的区域生成持久嵌入。该方法不裁剪输入或掩码特征，而是处理完整视频并将运动编码直接以查询区域为条件，从而在保留全局上下文（相机运动、场景布局）的同时隔离局部动态。实验证明该方法在对象级运动迁移和局部动作分类任务上优于通过裁剪或事后掩码获得的全局表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界动力学本质上是组合式的：同一场景中多个实体同时运动，各自呈现不同运动模式。然而现有视频表征大多全局编码运动，未显式捕获单个实体的局部运动。由于运动是相对于全局参考系（含相机运动与场景布局）定义的，而局部嵌入常通过裁剪图像或编码后掩码特征计算，会丢失解读运动所需的上下文信息。

Method: 提出可提示的局部运动表征：模型处理完整视频，直接以查询的掩码区域为条件进行运动编码，而非裁剪输入或掩码特征。该方法为指定区域生成时间一致、可区域寻址（region-addressable）的嵌入，在保留全局上下文用于消歧的同时隔离局部动力学。

Result: 在对象级运动迁移任务（可控动态场景组合）和局部动作分类任务（多演员视频）上，该方法均提升了可控性，并优于通过裁剪或事后掩码获得的全局表征。

Conclusion: 保留全局上下文对正确解读局部运动至关重要；以完整视频为输入并显式条件化于查询区域的方式，能够生成时间一致且可寻址的局部运动嵌入，支撑生成式控制与判别式分类两类下游任务。

Abstract: Real-world dynamics are inherently compositional: multiple entities move simultaneously within a shared scene, each exhibiting distinct motion patterns. Yet most existing video representations encode motion globally, without explicitly capturing localized motion for individual entities. Crucially, motion is defined relative to a global reference frame, including camera motion and scene layout. However, localized embeddings are often computed from cropped images or obtained by masking features after encoding, discarding the context needed to interpret motion. To address this, we introduce a promptable localized motion representation that produces persistent embeddings for user-specified regions defined by spatial masks. Rather than cropping the input or masking features, our model processes the full video and conditions motion encoding directly on the queried region. This yields temporally consistent, region-addressable embeddings that isolate local dynamics while retaining the global context required for disambiguation. We demonstrate object-level motion transfer, enabling controlled composition of dynamic scenes. Beyond generative control, our embeddings support localized action classification in multi-actor videos. Across both tasks, our approach improves controllability and outperforms global representations localized through cropping or post-hoc masking. Project Page: https://compvis.github.io/WhatMoves

</details>


### [10] [FAVE: Foveated Adaptive Visual Encoding for Efficient Fine-Grained Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04392)
*Amitangshu Mukherjee,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAVE是一种轻量级可变分辨率ViT，将"看哪里"与"编码什么"分离，仅对外部选定的局部区域进行高分辨率编码并保持原生几何结构。在受控小目标场景下，相比固定分辨率ViT在相同裁剪窗口上Top-1提升9.4个百分点且FLOPs降低12.7倍；集成到FastVLM作为互补局部分支后，仅用至多16个额外局部token即实现TextVQA提升1.60分、TTFT提速3.3倍，并在GQA属性问题上提升1.31分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度视觉理解依赖局部细节，但视觉编码器面临两难：全图高分辨率处理计算昂贵，而紧凑的全局编码会削弱局部证据。现有方法无法高效地同时兼顾全局表征与局部精细度，亟需一种能选择性分配高精度局部编码能力的机制。

Method: 借鉴人类主动视觉，FAVE将"看向哪里"（where to look）与"编码什么"（what to encode）解耦，专注后者。其核心是一个轻量级可变分辨率ViT，对外部选定的区域以高分辨率编码，同时保持原生几何结构。作者先用oracle真实标签裁剪在受控小目标（ImageNet中最大边≤96像素）场景验证编码问题本身，再将FAVE作为互补局部分支集成到FastVLM，其局部token与FastVLM全局token结合，而原有全局通路与语言模型保持冻结。

Result: 在受控小目标场景，FAVE较固定分辨率ViT在同一裁剪窗口上Top-1提升9.4分，FLOPs降低12.7倍，且提高全局分辨率或骨干容量无法达到相同工作点。在FastVLM集成中，至多16个额外局部token使TextVQA提升1.60分，较SmolVLM2-2.2B实现3.3倍受控TTFT加速；在GQA属性问题上较FastVLM-1.5B提升1.31分，缩小了与FastVLM-7B的差距。

Conclusion: 选择性分配高精度局部容量是全局表征与模型规模化的高效补充，能够显著改善小目标、文本与属性的细粒度理解，同时保持低计算开销。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual understanding depends on local detail, yet visual encoders face a trade-off between costly full-image high-resolution processing and compact global encoding that can weaken such evidence. Inspired by human active vision, we separate where to look from what to encode. We focus on the latter and introduce FAVE (Foveated Adaptive Visual Encoding), a lightweight variable-resolution ViT that encodes externally selected regions at high acuity while preserving native geometry. We first isolate this encoding problem using oracle ground-truth crops in a controlled small-object regime. On ImageNet objects with a native maximum side of 96 pixels, FAVE improves Top-1 by 9.4 points over a fixed-resolution ViT on the same crop window with 12.7 times lower FLOPs. Increasing global resolution or backbone capacity does not recover the same operating point. We then integrate FAVE as a complementary local branch in FastVLM. Its local tokens are combined with FastVLM's global visual tokens, while the original global pathway and language model remain frozen. With at most 16 additional local tokens, FAVE improves TextVQA by 1.60 points and achieves a 3.3 times controlled TTFT speedup over SmolVLM2-2.2B. On GQA attribute questions, it improves FastVLM-1.5B by 1.31 points, extending the benefit beyond text while narrowing the gap to FastVLM-7B. Together, these results show that selectively allocating high-acuity local capacity provides an efficient complement to broader global representations and model scaling for fine-grained understanding of small objects, text, and attributes.

</details>


### [11] [ICM-Bench: Person-Level Identity Reasoning in Multimodal Agents with Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04438)
*Shidu Ren,Yunze Liu,Xing Liu,Chi-Hao Wu,Enmin Zhou,Junxiao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ICM-Bench，据我们所知这是首个专门评估多模态智能体在长视频记忆中保持人物身份一致性并进行跨时间关系推理的基准。该基准包含839个合成片段（共141分钟）和1,217个开放式问题，围绕一年生活相册中的六位反复出现的成年人。评测发现Gemini 3.1 Pro整体准确率最高（74.0%），但在需要长期身份档案的问题上降至60.3%，表明现有系统在恢复事件级记忆方面表现较好，但在围绕稳定身份累积证据方面可靠性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程多模态智能体不仅应记住发生了什么，还应记住参与者是谁，这依赖于将反复出现的人脸、声音、名字、人物关联物体、事件和社会关系与跨时间的一致身份绑定。然而，现有长视频和多模态智能体基准仅衡量宽泛的记忆问答，未能隔离维持反复出现的身份并推理跨时间关系这一能力，因此需要一个专门针对身份中心推理的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建ICM-Bench基准：(1) 包含839个合成视频片段，总时长141分钟，含1,217个开放式问题，围绕六位反复出现的成年人在一年生活相册中的记录；(2) 采用主题可配置的流水线生成视频集合并将每个问题与其目标身份和可追踪的支持性证据关联；(3) 对比直接字幕记忆基线、记忆增强智能体和图检索系统等不同方法。

Result: Gemini 3.1 Pro在整体准确率上达到最高的74.0%，但在需要长期身份档案的问题上得分降至60.3%。实验表明当前系统能恢复许多事件级记忆，但当证据需要围绕稳定人物累积时，其可靠性明显下降。

Conclusion: 现有系统在事件级记忆恢复上表现较好，但在围绕稳定身份累积证据并进行跨时间推理方面仍不够可靠。ICM-Bench作为第一个身份中心记忆基准，为评估和推动多模态智能体在人物身份保持与跨时间关系推理方面的能力提供了专门的评测框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon multimodal agents should remember not only what happened but also who participated. This capability depends on linking recurring faces, voices, names, person-associated objects, events, and social relations to consistent identities over time. Existing long-video and multimodal-agent benchmarks measure broad memory question answering, but they do not isolate the ability to maintain recurring person identities and reason over their cross-time relations. We introduce ICM-Bench (Identity-Centric Memory Benchmark), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first benchmark specifically designed to evaluate identity-centric reasoning over long video memories in multimodal agents. The benchmark contains 839 synthetic clips spanning 141 minutes and 1,217 open-ended questions about six recurring adults in a one-year life album. A theme-configurable pipeline generates the video collection and associates each question with its target identities and traceable supporting evidence. We compare direct caption-memory baselines, memory-augmented agents, and graph-retrieval systems. Gemini 3.1 Pro achieves the highest overall accuracy of 74.0%, yet its score falls to 60.3% on questions that require long-term identity profiles. The results show that current systems recover many event-level memories but remain less reliable when evidence must be accumulated around a stable person.

</details>


### [12] [Segmentation of the aorta in 4D flow MRI using 4D convolutional kernels and learning from sparse annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04439)
*Hinrich Rahlfs,Julio Garcia,Chiara Manini,Markus Hüllebrand,Sebastian Schmitter,Sarah Nordmeyer,Titus Kühne,Heiko Stern,Christian Meierhofer,Andreas Harloff,Sebastian Kelle,Alexander Lenz,Peter Bannas,Jeanette Schulz-Menger,Ralf F Trauzeddel,Anja Hennemuth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 开发了一种基于参数高效混合4D内核的全自动4D U-Net，利用稀疏4D标签（来自现有2D专家轮廓和中心线）实现升主动脉、主动脉弓及近端降主动脉的时间分辨分割，在8中心2厂商268次扫描上训练，内外部测试集Dice分别达0.927和0.911，血流动力学指标与专家一致（ICC≥0.954），且模型公开可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D flow MRI中的自动化主动脉分割对于可重复的血流动力学评估至关重要，但受限于密集标注稀缺和高计算需求，现有方法（如逐帧3D网络、静态PC-MRA、基于配准的传播）在时间分辨率和准确度上存在不足。

Method: 构建4D（3D+时间）U-Net，采用参数高效的混合4D内核捕获时间上下文；利用从已有2D专家轮廓和中心线生成的稀疏4D标签训练，避免密集4D标注需求；训练集包含8个中心、2个厂商的268次扫描，并与逐帧3D网络及两种半自动参考方法对比。

Result: 4D U-Net在内部测试集（32例）和外部后对比剂测试集（30例）上Dice分别达0.927和0.911，优于3D U-Net（0.919/0.847）、静态PC-MRA（0.893）和基于配准的传播（0.808）；差异在收缩期小、舒张期明显；峰值流速、净流量、轴向/周向壁面剪切应力及直径等指标与专家轮廓一致性极佳（内部ICC≥0.954，外部≥0.980）。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了可重复、时间分辨的主动脉分割，实现全自动血流动力学分析，并能在多中心、多厂商及独立后对比剂数据上良好泛化；模型已公开发布。

Abstract: Automated aortic segmentation in 4D flow MRI is essential for reproducible hemodynamic assessment but is limited by scarce dense annotations and high computational demands. We developed a fully automated 4D (3D+time) U-Net for segmenting the ascending aorta, arch, and proximal descending aorta, using a parameter-efficient hybrid 4D kernel to capture temporal context and sparse 4D labels derived from existing 2D expert contours and centerlines, thereby avoiding the need for dense 4D annotations. Training comprised 268 scans from 8 centers and 2 vendors, with evaluation on an internal test set (32 scans) and an external post-contrast set (30 scans; different site, protocol, and annotator), compared against frame-wise 3D networks and two semi-automatic references. Against time-resolved annotations, the 4D U-Net achieved Dice scores of 0.927 (internal) and 0.911 (external), versus 0.919/0.847 for the 3D U-Net, 0.893 for static PC-MRA, and 0.808 for registration-based propagation; differences were small in systole but pronounced in diastole. Agreement with expert contours for peak velocity, net flow, axial and circumferential wall shear stress, and diameters was excellent (ICC >=0.954 internal, >=0.980 external), while semi-automatic references performed worse. The method thus provides reproducible, time-resolved aortic segmentation for automated hemodynamic analysis and generalizes across multicenter, multivendor, and independent post-contrast data. The model is publicly available.

</details>


### [13] [Topology-Aware Training and Spatial Diagnostics for Fiber Bundle Segmentation in Tracer Histology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04454)
*Joselyn Romero Avila,Kyriaki-Margarita Bintsi,Ermias Habte,Julia F. Lehman,Suzanne N. Haber,Anastasia Yendiki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将冻结的DINOv3基础模型特征用于猕猴示踪组织切片中的纤维束分割，系统比较了BCE-Dice、clDice、Betti匹配和Topograph四种损失函数，并引入新的空间诊断指标Excess32，证明仅依赖检测类指标（如TPR、Dice）无法充分刻画分割质量，存在过分割和空切片导致的召回虚高问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解剖示踪研究需要从组织切片中提取纤维束投影信息，用于验证扩散MRI纤维束成像。然而组织学数据的人工标注极为耗时，现有的自动分割方法主要依赖像素重叠类损失（BCE与Dice），缺乏针对该任务的拓扑感知损失函数研究，因此本文希望通过引入拓扑一致性与基础模型特征来提升分割质量。

Method: 采用冻结的DINOv3基础模型骨干，在猕猴示踪组织切片分割任务中对比四种损失函数：BCE-Dice、clDice、Betti匹配和Topograph；同时提出新的空间诊断指标Excess32——统计标注纤维束周围32像素容差带之外的预测像素数量，以量化过分割；并验证了Betti-Topograph损失联合的效果。

Result: BCE-Dice获得最高Dice；clDice获得最高束召回但掩膜重叠较差；Topograph的Dice与BCE-Dice相当，β0误差最低且假阳性少于BCE-Dice与Betti匹配。此外发现现有"任何重叠即视为检出"的宽松评价规则无法反映过分割，且逐切片TPR可被空切片的完美召回所虚高。验证中Betti-Topograph联合将稀疏束TPR从0.818提升至0.933，但FDR从0.296恶化至0.509、Excess32从0.108恶化至0.466、面积比从0.94恶化至3.34。

Conclusion: 研究表明，单纯依赖检出类指标（TPR、Dice等）不足以准确表征分割质量，必须辅以空间与拓扑诊断指标（如Excess32、FDR、面积比）来揭示过分割等问题；拓扑感知损与像素重叠损失的结合需在召回提升与精度/过分割之间进行权衡。

Abstract: Anatomic tracer studies reveal how axon bundles project from an injection site, branch into smaller groups of axons, and course through the brain to reach their destinations. Histological data from such studies provide anatomical reference information for validating diffusion MRI tractography. However, manual annotation of the histological data is very labor-intensive, and although automated segmentation methods have been proposed, they rely mainly on pixel-overlap losses such as BCE and Dice; topology-aware loss functions have not been studied for this task. We compare BCE-Dice, clDice, Betti matching, and Topograph for fiber bundle segmentation in macaque tracer histology using a frozen DINOv3 backbone. To our knowledge, this is the first exploration of foundation-model features for this task. BCE-Dice achieved the highest Dice, while clDice achieved the highest bundle recall but poor mask overlap. Topograph had similar Dice to BCE-Dice, the lowest $β_0$ error, and fewer false positives than BCE-Dice and Betti matching. Fiber bundle segmentation methods are typically evaluated with a permissive rule that counts a bundle as detected given any overlap with the prediction. We show this rule does not capture oversegmentation, and that per-section TPR can be inflated by empty sections assigned perfect recall. To quantify this, we introduce Excess32, a spatial diagnostic measuring predicted pixels outside a 32-pixel tolerance band around annotated bundles. In validation, a Betti-Topograph union raises sparse-bundle TPR from 0.818 to 0.933, but worsens FDR from 0.296 to 0.509, Excess32 from 0.108 to 0.466, and area ratio from 0.94 to 3.34. These results show detection metrics alone are insufficient to characterize segmentation quality.

</details>


### [14] [VISTA: Dense Multi-Label Classroom Coding with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04550)
*Andrew Franck,Brendan Ng,Ben Fitzgerald,Zane Derrod,Chris Cianci,Chris Craney*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将课堂观察协议COPUS（24个代码、多标签、十年信度文献支撑的观察工具）重构为多模态基础模型的视频基准，并发布VISTA基线：在冻结的MiniCPM-V-4.5骨干上以密集滑动窗口运行、叠加轻量MLP头精炼逐窗口输出、经最大池化映射到2分钟COPUS网格。在三个留出化学课上，VISTA受限宏准确率达80.1%，优于零样本变体的74.9%，并系统刻画了音频部分可观测性、细粒度小组工作判别和长尾召回三类失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言基准通常由数据集作者构建且未发布信度统计，其构造的噪声底限未知。本文主张多模态基准测试应借鉴那些已在确保信度方面投入策略的研究社区方法——通过将COPUS（具备十年同行评审信度文献、经外部验证的词汇表和逐代码人类评估者信度目标）重构为视频基准，为多模态基础模型提供高信度、结构密集的评估工具。

Method: 将COPUS重构为视频基准：输出为每2分钟一个24维二元向量，覆盖50-90分钟讲座的密集结构化标签。评估语料由5人评审小组生成，其共识矩阵作为参考。VISTA基线在冻结的MiniCPM-V-4.5上使用密集滑动窗口，以轻量MLP头精炼逐窗口输出，再经最大池化映射到2分钟COPUS网格。

Result: 在三个留出化学讲座上，VISTA达到80.1%受限宏准确率，相比零样本变体的74.9%有所提升；最大残余误差集中在视觉上相似的讲师代码和依赖音频的稀有代码上。论文刻画了三大系统性失效模式：音频部分可观测性、细粒度小组工作判别和长尾召回。基准工具、提示词和基线代码已开源。

Conclusion: 作者相信充分开发COPUS的信度文献体系能阐明当前视频语言基准中的噪声底限问题，VISTA作为基线验证了该基准的可行性，并指出了多模态模型在视听联合观测、细粒度群体行为判别和长尾类别召回上的改进方向。

Abstract: Video-language benchmarks are usually constructed by the dataset authors without published reliability statistics, leaving the noise floor of the construct unknown. We argue that multimodal benchmarking benefits from methods taken from research communities that have already invested in strategies to ensure reliability. We illustrate the case with the Classroom Observation Protocol for Undergraduate STEM (COPUS): a 24-code multi-label observation instrument with a decade of peer-reviewed reliability literature. We recast COPUS as a video benchmark for multimodal foundation models, where it provides a dense set of structured labels (a 24-dimensional binary vector every 2 minutes across a 50-90 minute lecture), an externally validated vocabulary, and established literature that provides a per-code reliability target based on human evaluators. Annotations in our evaluation corpus are produced by a 5-person human-evaluator panel whose consensus matrix is our reference. We propose VISTA, a baseline that runs MiniCPM-V-4.5 over a dense sliding window, refines its per-window outputs with a lightweight multi-layer perceptron (MLP) head trained on top of the frozen backbone, and max-pools the resulting predictions onto the 2-minute COPUS grid. On three held-out chemistry lectures, VISTA reaches 80.1% restricted macro accuracy versus 74.9% for the zero-shot variant, with the largest residual errors on visually similar instructor codes and on rare audio-dependent codes. We characterize three systematic failure modes (audio-partial observability, fine-grained group-work discrimination, long-tail recall) and release the benchmark tooling, prompts and baseline code at https://github.com/ajfranck/VISTA.

</details>


### [15] [DART: Depth-as-Target Pretraining for Surgical Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04555)
*John J. Han,Adam Schmidt,Muhammad Abdullah Jamal,Jie Ying Wu,Omid Mohareri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DART，一种基于 RGB-D 的预训练方案，在 DINOv2 基础上增加一个以伪标注深度为监督信号的像素空间深度重建目标（作用于被掩码的 iBOT patch）。深度仅在预训练阶段使用，微调和推理仍保持纯 RGB。在 8 个手术基准（分割、深度估计、图像级识别）上，DART 优于自然图像和域内基线，包括在相同数据上训练的原始 DINOv2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型在手术等数据稀缺领域价值显著，但主流自监督预训练仅使用 RGB 图像，忽略了深度图等可获取的互补信号。尤其在手术场景中，自然图像 VFM 迁移效果差，而场景几何信息丰富且具有价值。鉴于现有模型可为任意图像语料生成伪标注深度，作者假设可将这些信号纳入预训练以学习更好的表征。

Method: DART 建立在 DINOv2 之上，进行一项简单修改：针对被掩码的 iBOT patch 增加像素空间深度重建目标，并以伪标注的密集深度为监督信号。关键设计在于：深度仅用于预训练，不增加任何推理开销；同时通过对比实验（如与 Canny 边缘等替代密集信号比较）来验证深度几何信息的独特价值。

Result: 像素级重建头不但未破坏表征质量，反而提升了表征质量。深度作为目标比 Canny 边缘更有效，证明增益源于深度本身而非额外监督。在 8 个手术基准上，DART 优于自然图像与域内基线，包括同数据训练的原始 DINOv2，同时在密集预测和图像级理解两方面均有提升。

Conclusion: DART 表明，可自由获取的几何伪标签能够在无需额外标签或推理成本的情况下强化基础模型预训练，为手术场景提供了更强的骨干网络，验证了几何信息融入预训练的有效性。

Abstract: Vision foundation models (VFMs) are valuable in data-scarce domains such as surgery, where a single pretrained backbone can provide rich representations for many downstream tasks. Yet the dominant self-supervised pretraining paradigm uses only RGB images, leaving readily available complementary signals, such as depth maps, unused. This is a particular missed opportunity in surgery, where natural-image VFMs transfer poorly while the scene geometry is rich and informative. With strong off-the-shelf models now able to produce pseudo-labeled dense depth for any image corpus, we hypothesize that such signals can be folded into pretraining to learn better representations. We present DART, an RGB-D pretraining recipe that builds on DINOv2 with a simple modification: a pixel-space depth reconstruction objective applied to masked iBOT patches, supervised by pseudo-labeled depth. Depth is used only during pretraining, so fine-tuning and inference remain RGB-only. We find that this pixel-level reconstruction head improves representation quality rather than disrupting it. We further show that depth, which encodes scene geometry, is more effective as a target than alternative dense signals such as Canny edges, confirming that the gains stem from depth rather than added supervision alone. Across eight surgical benchmarks spanning segmentation, depth estimation, and image-level recognition, DART outperforms both natural-image and in-domain baselines, including a vanilla DINOv2 trained on identical data, improving dense prediction while also strengthening image-level understanding. More broadly, DART shows that freely available geometric pseudo-labels can strengthen foundation model pretraining without extra labels or added inference cost, pointing toward stronger backbones for surgery.

</details>


### [16] [Dual-Part Multi-Lateral Branched Network for Multi-Class Segmentation in Cardiovascular Catheterization Angiograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04590)
*Olatunji Omisore,Ahmed Elazab,Ali Shahidinejad,Fariza Sabrina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双部分MLBNet架构，用于心血管导管插入术场景中的多类别语义分割。该架构采用多侧编码器块和多头解码器分支，实现对导丝、导管、血管和背景像素的同时分类分割，并在多种模型数据（幻影模型、合成人模拟主动脉、动物模型）上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导管插入术图像处理需要快速、准确且可解释的分割模型。现有研究大多集中在二值分割上，但近期出现了对导管插入场景中多个结构同时分割的需求。为了满足这一需求，需要设计能够同时分割多个感兴趣结构的多类分割架构。

Method: 设计了一种双部分MLBNet架构：1) 编码器部分包含多侧（multi-lateral）编码器块，通过重复特征提取学习多样化的共享表示；2) 解码器部分包含多头（multi-head）解码器分支，引入类别偏斜（class-skewed）的分支以专门处理导管插入场景中不同结构的属性。使用来自幻影模型、合成人模拟主动脉和动物模型的多类分割血管造影数据进行训练和评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，双部分模型能够以高概率有效地将导丝、导管、血管和背景像素分割到其所属类别。所有模型都能从前景结构中区分主导的背景类别，并取得较高的整体精度。

Conclusion: 双部分MLBNet架构在多类别导管插入场景分割中表现有效，能够在区分主导背景类别的同时准确分割多个前景结构，满足导管插入术图像处理对快速、准确、可解释分割模型的需求。

Abstract: Catheterisation image processing requires segmentation models that are fast, accurate and explainable. While most of the existing studies usually focus on binary segmentation, there is a recent demand for simultaneous segmentation of multiple structures found in catheterization scenes. In this study, a dual-part MLBNet architecture is designed with multi-lateral encoder blocks and multi-head decoder branches for class-aware segmentation in cardiovascular catheterization scenes. Lateral branches in the encoder enables repeated feature extraction to learn diverse shared representations, while multiple decoder heads are used to introduce class-skewed branches that specialize in different structural properties in catheterization scenes. To analyze the performances of the dual-part MLBNet architecture, several multi-class segmentation angiogram data obtained during cardiovascular catheterization in phantom models, synthetic human-simulated aorta, and animal model are used for model training and evaluation. Results obtained showed the dual-part models could effectively separate guidewire, catheter, vessels and background pixels to their classes of memberships with high probability. The results demonstrate that all models were able to distinguish the dominant background class from foreground structures with high overall accuracy.

</details>


### [17] [Hidden In Plain Gaze: Gaze Representations as Privacy Controls for Utility and Re-identification Risk in XR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04592)
*Cory Ilo,Brendan-David John,Doug A. Bowman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究智能扩展现实(XR)系统中，注视数据表征方式的选择能否作为一种轻量级隐私控制手段。基于HoloAssist数据集，在相同模型容量下比较了原始注视、空间注意力热图和工程化眼动特征三种表征，分别评估其在动作识别(任务效用)和用户重识别(隐私泄露)上的表现。结果显示，工程化特征保留了原始注视约85%的动作识别准确率，同时将重识别率降低约一个数量级(约为206个身份下随机概率的4倍)，但对隐私的削弱而非消除，表明仅靠抽象化无法保证隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能XR系统日益依赖眼动和头部追踪来推断用户意图、任务和注意力，但同一信号也能泄露生物识别身份。现有隐私保护通常依赖扰动或正式隐私机制，作者希望探索在特征提取阶段、在添加扰动之前的轻量级隐私控制手段，即通过注视数据表征方式的选择来调节隐私-效用权衡。

Method: 使用第一人称视角的HoloAssist数据集，在匹配模型容量的条件下对比三种注视表征：原始注视(原始数据)、空间注意力热图(空间聚合表征)、工程化眼动特征(基于领域知识提取的度量特征)。将每种表征分别用于动作识别(作为任务效用指标)和封闭集用户重识别(作为隐私泄露指标)，以此评估表征选择对隐私-效用权衡的影响。

Result: 表征选择显著改变隐私-效用权衡。工程化特征保留原始注视约85%的动作识别准确率，同时将重识别率降低约一个数量级，达到约206个身份下随机概率(机会率)的4倍。这表明隐私泄露被削弱但未被消除，且各表征间的差异证明仅靠抽象化并不能保证隐私。

Conclusion: 工程化特征比原始注视和热图更能平衡隐私与效用，且具有可解释、可审计的结构，为设计者提供了透明且轻量级的隐私控制杠杆，可与差分隐私等正式机制互补使用。但研究也警示，抽象化本身不足以消除身份泄露，隐私保护仍需多层次的综合手段。

Abstract: Intelligent extended reality (XR) systems increasingly use eye and head tracking to infer user intent, task, and attention, but the same signals can also reveal biometric identity. We study whether gaze data representation choice can serve as a lightweight privacy control at feature extraction, before adding perturbation or formal privacy mechanisms. Using the egocentric HoloAssist dataset, we compare three gaze representations under matched model capacity: raw gaze, spatial attention heatmaps, and engineered eye-movement features. We evaluate each representation on action recognition as task utility and closed-set user re-identification as privacy leakage. Representation choice substantially changes the privacy-utility tradeoff. Engineered features retain roughly 85% of raw gaze's action-recognition accuracy while reducing re-identification by about an order of magnitude, to roughly four times the chance rate across 206 identities. This reduction attenuates rather than eliminates identity leakage, and the differences across representations show that abstraction alone does not guarantee privacy. Engineered features expose interpretable and auditable structure, giving designers a transparent privacy lever that complements mechanisms such as differential privacy.

</details>


### [18] [An Evaluation Framework for Generating Multi-View Images of a Person in a Scene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04603)
*Mahir Majid,Young Kyung Kim,Guillermo Sapiro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式图像编辑DiT模型在相机角度空间一致变化方面的不足，指出其瓶颈在于缺乏人类主体在自然场景中固定位置的多视角配对数据。作者先尝试用SOTA编辑模型合成此类数据，但发现输出常出现头部转向与背景不一致的幻觉。为此提出HSRD（Head Scene Rotation Difference）指标，通过解耦相机移动与局部头部姿态操控来定量评估围绕人物的相机运动，为可靠构建高质量多视角合成数据提供管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式图像编辑DiT模型虽具备强大的语义编辑能力，但在空间一致的相机角度变化上仍有困难。核心瓶颈是缺乏专门的训练数据——即人类主体在固定场景中包括正面和侧面的多视角配对数据。在非受限环境中拍摄此类多相机数据在物流上困难且不可扩展，因此需要其他途径获得这类数据。

Method: 作者首先实验多种SOTA图像编辑模型以合成多视角数据，但发现输出常出现幻觉，如主体头部转动幅度与背景不一致，导致环境不一致。在此基础上提出HSRD（Head Scene Rotation Difference）指标，通过将相机移动与局部头部姿态操控解耦，定量评估围绕人物发生的相机运动，用于衡量3D空间视差。

Result: 大量实验表明，HSRD为人物在场景中的3D空间视差提供了必要的评估管线，为可靠构建高质量多视角合成数据集奠定基础。

Conclusion: HSRD指标能够有效评估围绕人物的相机运动，解决合成数据中头部转动与背景不一致的幻觉问题，从而可靠地构建多视角合成数据集，支持训练具备一致相机角度变化能力的DiT模型。

Abstract: Recent generative image-editing Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) demonstrate impressive semantic editing capabilities but still struggle with spatially consistent camera angle changes. A primary bottleneck in training foundation models to execute free-form, promptable camera angle changes is the lack of specialized training data. While multi-view datasets exist for generic 3D environments and objects, there remains an absence of paired, multi-view datasets featuring human subjects at fixed locations in natural scenes, including frontal and side-profile views. Capturing such multi-camera data in unconstrained environments is logistically challenging and unscalable. In this paper, we first experiment with multiple state-of-the-art image editing models to create this data synthetically, but find that the outputs are frequently prone to hallucinations involving how much the subject's head turns relative to the background, often producing inconsistent environments. To address this issue, we propose the Head Scene Rotation Difference (HSRD) metric to quantitatively evaluate camera movements around a person. The proposed metric operates by decoupling camera movement from localized head pose manipulation. As demonstrated by the extensive experimentation, HSRD provides the pipeline necessary to evaluate 3D spatial parallax for a person in a scene, paving the way to reliably construct high-quality multi-view synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [19] [Importance-Aware Low-Rank Distillation of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04646)
*Denis Zavadski,Sebastian Heid,Damjan Kalšan,Stefan Roth,Carsten Rother*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SVDtrunc，一种基于截断SVD的两步式块级压缩方法，用于高效压缩扩散变压器（DiT），在FLUX.dev上实现40-90%的参数压缩，并在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT虽为高质量文本到图像生成的主流架构，但模型规模庞大导致部署困难。截断SVD虽有降参潜力，但LLM领域的经验表明朴素低秩近似会引发灾难性性能失败。作者发现DiT中截断SVD的退化是平滑的，且冗余分布在整个网络的投影矩阵中，而非集中于少数Transformer块，这为压缩提供了新契机。

Method: 提出两步式块级压缩方案SVDtrunc：第一步在全局参数预算下，跨块分配秩，并采用截断SVD压缩最不重要的块；第二步利用模块化知识蒸馏与整流流目标对所有块进行微调。

Result: 在GenEval、HPSv2和DPG三个基准上，SVDtrunc优于所有对比方法。在保留原参数68%时仍接近完整性能，在保留57%时仍具竞争力。该方法还能与步进蒸馏互补，且在不进行微调时也能取得良好效果。

Conclusion: SVDtrunc为大规模生成模型提供了一种超越扩散步数缩减的实用效率提升途径，可作为步进蒸馏的补充手段，推动高效部署。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have emerged as a dominant architecture for high-quality text-to-image generation, yet their scale poses challenges for efficient deployment. While truncated singular value decomposition (SVD) is a principled tool for parameter reduction, evidence from large language models (LLMs) suggests that naive low-rank approximation can cause catastrophic failure. In contrast, we find that truncated SVD in DiTs produces smooth degradation even under substantial global compression, with redundancy distributed across projection matrices throughout the whole network rather than concentrated in a few transformer blocks. Building on these insights, we introduce SVDtrunc, a two-step block-level compression scheme, first allocating ranks across blocks and compressing the least important ones via truncated SVD under a global parameter budget, and then fine-tuning all blocks with modular knowledge distillation and a rectified-flow objective. We apply SVDtrunc to FLUX.dev across compression levels ranging from 40-90% of the original parameter count. Across three benchmarks, GenEval, HPSv2, and DPG, we outperform all competing approaches. Notably, and in contrast to prior work, we retain near-full performance at 68% and remain competitive even at 57% of the original parameter budget. Furthermore, we show that SVDtrunc complements step distillation and achieves strong results even without fine-tuning, positioning it as a practical continuation of efficiency improvements beyond diffusion step reduction for large-scale generative models.
  Project page: https://vislearn.github.io/SVDtrunc/

</details>


### [20] [ReaDiT Guidance: Control for Image and Video Generation using Diffusion Transformer Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04649)
*Jay Mahajan,Chang Liu,Rauf Makharov,Viraj Shah,Alexander Schwing,Svetlana Lazebnik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReaDiT Guidance（DiT Readout Guidance），一种轻量级框架，利用扩散Transformer（DiT）模型内部的特征表示来控制生成过程。该框架仅使用单个DiT块的特征，依据测试时给定的空间目标（如深度图、姿态、边缘图）来引导生成，并自然扩展到视频生成，实现相机和运动控制。实验表明，与现有基于特征的方法及现成适配器方法相比，该方法在参数更少的情况下取得了具有竞争力或更优的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于特征的控制方法通常需要大量参数或复杂的适配结构来引导DiT模型生成。作者旨在开发一种轻量级的特征读取（readout）机制，仅利用DiT模型内部已有的丰富特征表示（而非额外插入大量可训练模块），实现对生成过程的精准空间控制，同时大幅降低参数开销。

Method: 核心思想是"特征读取"（readout）：直接从单个DiT块中提取内部特征，并据此构建一个轻量指导信号，将测试时提供的空间条件（深度、姿态、边缘等）注入到生成采样过程中。由于现代文生视频模型大多基于DiT骨干，该方法可复用同一机制，通过对视频帧特征进行读取来实现相机与运动控制。

Result: 实验结果显示，ReaDiT Guidance在图像和视频生成任务上均取得了与现有基于特征的方法及现成适配器方法相当甚至更优的生成质量，同时所需的可训练参数显著更少。

Conclusion: 证明了利用DiT内部特征作为轻量级控制读取点是可行且高效的方案，为图像与视频生成提供了统一、参数高效的条件控制范式，有望替代更重的适配器架构。

Abstract: We present DiT Readout (ReaDiT) Guidance, a lightweight framework for controlling generation with Diffusion Transformer (DiT) models via their internal feature representations. ReaDiT Guidance uses features from a single DiT block to steer the generative process according to spatial targets - like depth, pose, or edge maps - provided at test time. Furthermore, since modern text-to-video models are largely built on DiT backbones, ReaDiT Guidance naturally extends to video generation, enabling camera and motion control. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive or improved results compared to existing feature-based and off-the-shelf adapter-based approaches while requiring fewer parameters.

</details>


### [21] [Retinal OCTA Phenotyping with LLM Reporting for Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04689)
*Progga Paromita Dutta,Jeba Maliha,Md Rafiul Kabir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种可解释的OCTA（光学相干断层扫描血管成像）分析流水线，用于阿尔茨海默病（AD）的早期筛查研究。流水线整合了注释感知的血管分割、分层血管生物标志物提取、无标签表型分析以及基于测量结果的大语言模型（LLM）报告。使用来自39名受试者的117张ROSE-1图像，分割模型在浅表血管复合体（SVC）、深层血管复合体（DVC）及组合表示上取得ROC-AUC 0.916-0.970和Dice 0.695-0.781。六种密度与分形维数生物标志物构成受试者层面的特征用于探索性聚类，并在九名留出受试者中识别出内部一致的低密度、低分形维数表型，但因缺乏诊断标签无法进行临床解读。GPT、Gemini和Llama生成的报告在测量依据、引用忠实度和诊断谨慎性方面得到评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病的早期识别仍具挑战性，因为既有的评估方法成本高、资源密集或不适合人群规模筛查。OCTA可无创观察视网膜微血管，但现有方法通常需要诊断标签且缺乏测量层面的解释能力。因此需要一种无需标签、可解释且面向测量结果的OCTA分析框架。

Method: 提出一个可解释OCTA流水线，包含四个模块：1）注释感知的血管分割（应用于SVC、DVC及组合SVC+DVC表示）；2）基于分层的血管生物标志物提取（六种密度和分形维数指标）；3）无标签表型分析（探索性聚类形成受试者层面的特征图谱）；4）测量驱动的LLM报告生成（使用GPT、Gemini、Llama），并评估其测量依据、引用忠实度和诊断谨慎性。

Result: 使用117张来自39名受试者的ROSE-1图像，分割模型达到ROC-AUC 0.916-0.970、Dice 0.695-0.781。对九名留出受试者的分析识别出内部一致的低密度、低分形维数表型，但由于缺乏诊断标签无法进行临床解读。LLM生成报告在测量依据、引用忠实度与诊断谨慎性维度上得到评价。

Conclusion: 该框架为视网膜血管测量、探索性表型分析与基于证据的解读之间提供了一条透明且非诊断性的连接路径，适用于阿尔茨海默病研究，可作为大规模人群筛查和科研探索的参考工具。

Abstract: Early identification of Alzheimer's disease (AD) remains challenging because established assessment methods can be costly, resource-intensive, or unsuitable for population-scale screening. Optical coherence tomography angiography (OCTA) provides non-invasive visualization of retinal microvasculature, but existing approaches often require diagnostic labels and provide limited measurement-level interpretation. We present an explainable OCTA pipeline that integrates annotation-aware vessel segmentation, layer-specific vascular biomarker extraction, label-free phenotyping, and measurement-grounded LLM reporting. Using 117 ROSE-1 images from 39 subjects, we apply annotation-matched segmentation models to superficial vascular complex (SVC), deep vascular complex (DVC), and combined SVC+DVC representations. The models achieve ROC-AUC values of 0.916-0.970 and Dice scores of 0.695-0.781. Six density and fractal-dimension biomarkers form subject-level profiles for exploratory clustering. Analysis of nine held-out subjects identifies an internally consistent lower-density, lower-fractal-dimension phenotype, although the absence of diagnostic labels prevents clinical interpretation. Reports generated using GPT, Gemini, and Llama are evaluated for measurement grounding, citation faithfulness, and diagnostic caution. Overall, the framework provides a transparent, non-diagnostic connection between retinal vascular measurements, exploratory phenotyping, and evidence-linked interpretation for Alzheimer's research.

</details>


### [22] [Enhancing Multimodal Emotion Recognition via Multi-Feature Encoding and Attention-Based Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04690)
*Xu Lin,Ke Wang,Hui Kang,Xinying Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态情绪识别框架，融合音频特征（Wav2Vec2语义嵌入、MFCC、声学统计描述符，经BiLSTM处理）与视频特征（ResNet50-BiLSTM提取时空特征），并采用基于多头注意力的特征级融合策略，在MELD和IEMOCAP数据集上显著优于基线，在非平衡数据场景下表现尤其突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情绪识别在人机交互、远程教育和医疗健康等应用中日益重要。现有方法在音频与视觉特征的互补提取以及模态间协同融合方面仍存在不足，尤其是面对非平衡数据时融合策略的有效性问题，促使作者提出更稳健的多模态框架。

Method: 音频侧提取三类互补特征：Wav2Vec2语义嵌入、MFCC和统计声学描述符（音高、能量、韵律），经对齐后由BiLSTM捕获时序依赖；视频侧采用ResNet50-BiLSTM架构，结合深度残差学习与序列建模提取面部序列的时空特征；最后通过基于多头注意力的特征级融合机制自适应地加权各模态贡献，实现多模态协同。

Result: 在MELD和IEMOCAP两个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在准确率和鲁棒性方面均显著优于基线模型。消融实验进一步证明注意力融合策略在非平衡数据设置下能显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效捕捉语音和面部视觉表达中的多样情绪线索，提供了一种实用且可泛化的多模态情绪识别方案，适用于真实世界场景。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition has attracted growing interest due to its importance in human-computer interaction, remote education, and healthcare. This paper proposes a novel multimodal emotion recognition framework that integrates rich audio and visual feature extraction with an attention-based fusion strategy. For audio, we extract three complementary feature types: semantic embeddings from Wav2Vec2, MFCC features, and statistical acoustic descriptors such as pitch, energy, and rhythm. These are aligned and fused via a BiLSTM to capture temporal dependencies. For video, we propose a ResNet50-BiLSTM architecture that combines deep residual learning and sequential modeling to extract expressive spatiotemporal features from facial sequences. To enhance multimodal synergy, we introduce a feature-level fusion mechanism based on multi-head attention, allowing the model to adaptively weigh contributions across modalities. Experiments conducted on the MELD and IEMOCAP datasets demonstrate that our model significantly outperforms baselines in both accuracy and robustness. Furthermore, ablation studies show that the attention-based fusion strategy significantly improves performance in unbalanced data settings. Our findings suggest that the proposed framework effectively captures diverse emotional cues from speech and visual expressions, and offers a practical and generalizable approach for real-world multimodal emotion recognition tasks.

</details>


### [23] [LookThere! Sparse Vision by Reinforced Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04698)
*Sreehari Rammohan,Yousef Yassin,Anthony Fuller,Junfeng Wen,Carl Vondrick,Evan Shelhamer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LookThere提出了一种基于端到端强化学习的自适应计算框架，通过联合训练浅层输入选择器和深层表征提取器，实现了性能与计算量的帕累托最优权衡。该模型仅选择对任务有用的输入区域，在仅使用0.2%输入的情况下仍能保持准确性，并在全局识别、局部识别、零样本分类和回归等多种任务中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉Transformer通常将所有图像token视为同等重要，但实际上大多数任务只依赖其中一小部分。现有自适应计算方法（如token多样性、注意力分数等启发式策略）在极端稀疏场景下表现不佳，且依赖可能无法泛化的启发式信号。作者旨在克服这些局限，实现无需辅助信号、可推广到多种任务的通用高效自适应计算框架。

Method: LookThere采用端到端强化学习框架，联合训练两个模块：一个浅层输入选择器负责决定"看哪里"，一个深层表征提取器负责"看什么"。选择器学习选取对特定任务有价值的输入区域，提取器学习从选中区域提取表征，二者协同节省计算量，且不依赖任何辅助信号。该框架具有任务和模型的泛化能力，并支持通过蒸馏实现零样本分类。

Result: LookThere在性能-计算权衡上取得了新的帕累托前沿。它能够仅选择任务相关的输入，在高分辨率下的稀疏识别场景（如交通标志、台球）表现出色，仅用0.2%输入即可保持准确率。在全局识别（ImageNet分类）、局部识别（ADE20K分割）、零样本分类（蒸馏）和回归（计数）等任务中均超越最新方法。

Conclusion: LookThere提供了一个通用且可扩展的自适应计算框架，在极端稀疏条件下仍能保持高精度，并显著超越现有token选择方法，适用于专业化和高效的自适应计算需求，具有良好的任务和模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision transformers typically treat every image token as equally important, yet for most tasks in computer vision only a fraction are needed. Adaptive computation methods accelerate inference by choosing which tokens to process, but existing methods struggle at extreme sparsity and require heuristics that may not generalize like token diversity and attention scores. We address these limitations with LookThere, achieving a new pareto frontier in performance-compute trade-offs through an end-to-end reinforcement learning framework that jointly trains a shallow input selector and a deep representation extractor. The selector learns where to look and the extractor learns what to see, together saving computation by selecting only what is worth processing for a given task without relying on auxiliary signals. We show that LookThere only selects the task-specific input, excelling at sparse recognition in high-resolution settings (traffic signs, billiards), and maintaining accuracy with as little as 0.2% of the input. It generalizes across tasks and models, including global recognition (ImageNet classification), local recognition (ADE20K segmentation), zero-shot classification (by distillation), and regression (counting). Across all settings, LookThere surpasses state-of-the-art selection to provide a general and scalable framework for specialized and efficient adaptive computation.

</details>


### [24] [AngelFingerprint: A Traceable, Explainable, and White-Box Stealthy Watermark for Text-Guided Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04709)
*Bo-Han Kung,Futa Waseda,Ching-Chun Chang,Isao Echizen,Shang-Tse Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AngelFingerprint，一种面向文本引导扩散编辑的水印框架，通过将编辑提示的CLIP文本嵌入整合进模型权重（LoRA）而非独立模块，实现编辑可溯源、可解释和自盒隐身性，并可在图像像素级别恢复语义载荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导的扩散编辑引发虚假信息担忧，可靠的图像溯源至关重要。现有水印方法携带固定ID，无法解释"改了什么"以及"用哪个提示生成"。更重要的是，在开源白盒访问下，攻击者能轻易定位并移除以独立模块形式添加的水印。因此需要一种具备编辑可追溯性、可解释性以及白盒隐身性的水印方法。

Method: 将LoRA集成到扩散模型中，直接将编辑提示的CLIP文本嵌入植入模型权重；随后一个提取器仅从图像像素中恢复该嵌入，实现语义载荷的恢复。采用两种关键技术：速度对齐锚点（velocity-alignment anchor）以保持编辑质量，以及专门设计的频率滤波器保持水印不可感知但可恢复且鲁棒。

Result: 在MagicBrush数据集上，所提提取器在200路提示检索中达到86%的top-1准确率，而提示反转（prompt inversion）方法仅20%。

Conclusion: 权重视图集成式水印设计使水印难以在白盒访问下被检测和分离，同时语义载荷能解释编辑内容，兼顾溯源、可解释性与隐身性，显著优于提示反转基线。

Abstract: Text-guided diffusion editing raises disinformation concerns, making reliable image provenance essential. While watermarks are commonly used for this purpose, most methods carry a fixed ID that cannot explain what was changed and which prompt produced it. Furthermore, under open-source white-box access, attackers can easily locate and remove watermarks added as separate modules. Targeting this setting, we propose AngelFingerprint, a novel watermarking framework ensuring edit traceability, explainability, and white-box stealthiness. It integrates a LoRA into the diffusion model to embed the editing prompt's CLIP text embedding directly into the model's weights. An extractor then recovers this embedding from the image pixels alone. This semantic payload explains the edit, while the weight-integrated design makes it hard to detect and isolate even under full white-box access. Two techniques make this possible: a velocity-alignment anchor that preserves edit quality, and a specially designed frequency filter that keeps the watermark imperceptible yet recoverable and robust. On the MagicBrush dataset, our extractor achieves $86\%$ top-1 accuracy in a 200-way prompt retrieval, versus $20\%$ for prompt inversion.

</details>


### [25] [Counting Beyond Instances: A Benchmark for Group-Individual Object Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04716)
*Rui Wang,Junyi Huang,Jiahui Li,Qiao Yu,Yixue Hao,Long Hu,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个全新的计数任务——群体-个体目标计数（GIC），要求模型在同一框架内同时统计语义群体（如一串葡萄、一叠盘子）和个体对象。为此构建了真实基准BunchCount（1,330张图像、89,254个个体标注、11,065个群体标注，含配对标注和包含关系），并提出一种基于计数单元引导的关系型计数框架，利用群体-个体包含关系来正则化跨粒度表示，在群体级计数上显著提升，同时保持个体级计数能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉计数任务基本在实例层面展开，只关注"数什么"（目标类别），却忽略了"以什么语义单元来数"。现实世界中常见的计数对象往往是多个实例构成的高层语义单元（如葡萄串、盘子叠、鞋对），这种语义粒度冲突暴露了现有计数范式的主要局限。

Method: 提出GIC新任务及双策略：(1) 构建BunchCount基准，提供同图内的配对个体-群体标注，并显式记录群体与所含个体的包含关系；(2) 提出计数单元引导的关系型计数框架，通过群体-个体包含关系在训练期间对跨粒度表示进行正则化，缓解语义粒度冲突。

Result: 在BunchCount上的实验表明，现有先进的计数模型在个体实例上表现良好，但在语义群体计数上表现差强人意；所提出的方法在群体级计数上取得显著提升，同时更好地保留了个体级计数能力，为超越实例的计数建立了强基线。

Conclusion: 该工作突破了传统计数仅聚焦"数什么"的局限，引入"以何种语义单元计数"的全新维度，通过配对标注的基准构建和关系型跨粒度正则化，有效弥合了个体计数与群体计数之间的鸿沟，为更高层语义单元的通用计数提供了新方向。

Abstract: Visual counting is commonly formulated at the instance level, aiming to estimate how many objects of a queried category appear in an image. However, real-world counting often involves higher-level semantic units formed by multiple instances, such as a bunch of grapes, a stack of plates, or a pair of shoes. This exposes a key limitation of existing counting formulations, which mainly focus on what to count, while largely overlooking at which semantic unit to count. We introduce Group-Individual Object Counting (GIC), a new setting that requires models to count both individual objects and semantic groups within a unified framework. To support this new task, we present BunchCount, a real-world benchmark with 1,330 images, 89,254 individual annotations, and 11,065 group annotations. BunchCount provides paired individual-group annotations within the same image and explicitly records containment relations between each group and its constituent individuals. Experiments on BunchCount show that current advanced counting models perform well on individual instances but fail to count semantic groups more accurately. To mitigate semantic granularity conflict, we propose a counting-unit guided relational counting framework, which exploits group-individual containment relations to regularize cross-granularity representations during training. Our method substantially improves group-level counting while better preserving individual-level counting ability, establishing a strong baseline for counting beyond instances.

</details>


### [26] [HiSfM: Disambiguating Structure-from-Motion via Scaffold-Anchored Hierarchical Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04718)
*Ziding Zhao,Hainan Cui,Peilin Tao,Shuhan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiSfM 是一个分层粗到细的 SfM 框架，通过脚手架（scaffold）构建来提升鲁棒性和效率。它先用几何启发式形成强局部社区，再用边不相交生成树（EDST）连接社区形成紧凑骨架，并用双目消歧器验证骨架边，重建稳定脚手架后吸收剩余图像完成精细重建，有效防止歧义导致的失败并大幅降低计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 SfM 管线在面对重复或对称结构造成的视觉歧义时常常失败，且因冗余相机和约束带来高昂计算成本。现有方法难以在保证鲁棒性的同时兼顾效率，亟需一种既能防歧义失败又轻量的重建框架。

Method: HiSfM 采用层次化粗到细策略：首先依据几何诱导启发式构建强局部社区；然后利用边不相交生成树（EDST）打包方式连接社区得到紧凑而强健的骨架，并用双目消歧器对骨架边进行验证；在验证后的骨架上重建稳定的脚手架作为场景锚点，最终通过高效配准和三角化吸收剩余图像并做进一步细化。

Result: 在面向歧义的基准测试和通用数据集上的实验表明，HiSfM 能有效防止歧义诱发的重建失败，相比先前方法大幅缩短运行时间，并且相比激进稀疏化方法在完整性上也有提升。

Conclusion: HiSfM 通过分层脚手架结构兼顾了 SfM 的鲁棒性与效率，为解决视觉歧义场景下的重建难题提供了可行方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Structure-from-Motion (SfM) is a fundamental tool for sparse 3D reconstruction with broad impact in robotics and vision, supporting mapping, localization, and large-scale scene modeling. However, conventional pipelines often fail under hard visual ambiguity caused by repeated or symmetric structures, and incur heavy computational cost due to redundant cameras and constraints. We present HiSfM, a hierarchical coarse-to-fine SfM framework that improves robustness and efficiency through scaffold construction. HiSfM first forms strong local communities using geometrical induced heuristics, then connects communities with a compact yet strong skeleton by packing edge-disjoint spanning trees (EDST) while verifying skeletal edges with a two-view disambiguator. We reconstruct a stable scaffold on this verified skeleton, serving as an anchor to capture the essence of the scene, and subsequently absorb remaining images via efficient registration and triangulation for further refinements. Experiments on ambiguity-focused benchmarks and general datasets show that HiSfM prevents ambiguity-induced failures while substantially reducing runtime compared to previous methods, and improves completeness over aggressive sparsification methods. Code is available at https://github.com/3dv-casia/HiSfM.

</details>


### [27] [Bridging Modalities and Tasks: A Unified Hierarchical ViT for SAR-to-Optical Translation and Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04726)
*Siyuan Liu,Xuze Zhang,Yongshun Wang,Licong Pan,Hang Liu,Huihui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一协同双任务学习框架BMT，通过共享层次化Vision Transformer联合优化SAR-光学图像翻译（S2O）与语义分割，并在配对与非配对设置下均取得有竞争力的翻译质量和分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR图像具有全天候、昼夜观测能力，但其斑点噪声和非直观散射机制限制了可解释性。现有的S2O生成模型往往为追求视觉效果而忽略语义结构的约束，而语义结构是下游任务所必需的，因此需要一种在翻译与分割之间进行协同优化的框架。

Method: 提出BMT框架，包含四个核心组件：(1) LocalViTBlock，通过可学习门控机制融合全局自注意力与空间深度卷积；(2) 增强输出模块，结合多尺度细化、色彩校正与抗锯齿，并通过特征融合校准通道级色彩统计；(3) ControlNet式条件注入机制，将SAR小波特征与分割标签编码为多尺度特征金字塔，并通过零初始化卷积注入到各编码器层；(4) 有界Kendall不确定性加权方案，防止任一任务主导共享表征。在有配对（WHU-OPT-SAR）与无配对（自建基于HRSID和DIOR的船舶数据集）设置下评估。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法在S2O翻译质量和语义分割性能上均取得有竞争力的结果，数据集和源代码已公开。

Conclusion: 通过双任务协同学习与条件注入机制，BMT能够在提升SAR图像视觉可解释性的同时保持语义结构约束，兼顾翻译质量与下游分割任务，为跨模态遥感图像转换提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) images have all-weather, day-and-night observation capabilities. However, compared with optical images, their speckle noise and non-intuitive scattering mechanism limit the interpretability of the images. Generative models for SAR-to-optical (S2O) conversion can improve visual interpretability, but existing methods often ignore the constraints on semantic structure, which are necessary for downstream tasks, for the sake of visual effects. We propose a unified collaborative dual-task learning framework, termed BMT (Bridging Modalities and Tasks), that jointly optimizes S2O image translation and semantic segmentation through a shared hierarchical Vision Transformer. The framework integrates: (1) a LocalViTBlock that fuses global self-attention with spatial depthwise convolution through a learnable gating mechanism; (2) an enhanced output module combining multi-scale refinement processing, color correction and anti-aliasing, which calibrates channel-level color statistics through feature fusion; (3) a ControlNet-style conditional injection mechanism that encodes SAR wavelet features and segmentation labels into a multi-scale feature pyramid and injects them at each encoder layer through zero-initialized convolution; (4) a bounded Kendall uncertainty weighting scheme that prevents either task from dominating the shared representation. We evaluate the framework under both paired and unpaired translation settings, on the public WHU-OPT-SAR paired dataset and a self-constructed unpaired ship dataset built from HRSID and DIOR, respectively. The experimental results show that the proposed method achieves competitive S2O translation quality and semantic segmentation performance. The dataset and source code have been publicly released at https://github.com/Lewisyuaner/BMT-S2O-main.

</details>


### [28] [Where to Look Matters: Learning Influential Views for VLM-based 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04741)
*Tsung-Chih Chiang,Hsuan-Kung Yang,Jou-Min Liu,Ting-Ru Liu,Chun-Wei Huang,Quan Kong,Chun-Yi Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IVSGround框架，通过学习“影响性视图选择”替代启发式规则，让轻量级视图选择器为每个候选目标生成查询相关的关键视图，再由冻结的推理VLM进行对比式接地，从而显著提升零样本3D视觉接地的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本3D视觉接地方法依赖启发式规则选择相机视图（往往只关注目标可见性），忽视了视图对“接地相关性”的影响，导致选出的视图缺乏判别性证据，从而限制了VLM的定位性能。

Method: 1) 训练轻量级视图选择器替代固定启发式规则；2) 通过两阶段拒绝采样，利用推理VLM的反馈生成训练监督信号；3) 推理阶段由学习到的选择器为每个候选目标预测查询条件化的影响性视图；4) 所选视图交由冻结的推理VLM进行对比式接地。

Result: 在ScanRefer和NR3D数据集上的实验表明，IVSGround在接地准确率上持续优于现有零样本流程，证明“看哪里”对3D视觉接地至关重要。

Conclusion: 学习式视图选择相较固定启发式更有效，影响性视图的选择是提升VLM零样本3D视觉接地性能的关键环节。

Abstract: Recent zero-shot 3D visual grounding methods leverage vision-language models (VLMs) to localize objects in 3D scenes from natural language queries. However, these methods typically rely on heuristic rules to select which camera views are provided to the VLM, often prioritizing object visibility rather than grounding relevance. We present IVSGround, a framework that learns Influential View Selection for VLM-based 3D visual grounding. Instead of using fixed heuristics, a lightweight view selector is trained to identify views that provide discriminative evidence for grounding. To obtain supervision signals, we generate training signals using feedback from a reasoning VLM through a two-stage rejection sampling process. During inference, the learned selector predicts query-conditioned influential views for each candidate object, which are then evaluated by a frozen reasoning VLM through comparative grounding. Experiments on ScanRefer and NR3D show that IVSGround consistently improves grounding accuracy over existing zero-shot pipelines, demonstrating that selecting where to look is crucial for effective 3D visual grounding. Project page: https://ivsground.github.io/

</details>


### [29] [SeamFlow: Structure-Aware Flow Matching on Edge Probabilities for Artist-Like UV Unwrapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04751)
*Yuming Zhao,Zangyueyang Xian,Qijian Zhang,Rendong Liang,Qin Jia,Ying He,Junhui Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeamFlow 是一个用于三维表面切割/UV展开的生成式框架，将离散的网格切割问题重构为高维边概率空间中的连续流匹配，通过演化网络耦合局部拓扑标记与全局形状先验，并借助常微分方程求解引导概率流，在提升语义连贯性的同时显著降低参数化畸变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维表面切割与UV展开是计算机图形学的基础问题。传统几何优化方法主要追求降低参数化畸变，但常忽略接缝布局的视觉语义连贯性；而近年自回归生成方法虽提升了语义连贯性，却因对网格拓扑感知有限，常导致局部切割不准确。为此需要一种兼顾拓扑感知与语义连贯性的新方法。

Method: 将离散网格切割问题重构为高维边概率空间中的连续流匹配，通过连续松弛学习从高斯先验到目标接缝概率分布的确定性映射；设计演化网络将局部拓扑标记与全局形状先验耦合，并通过常微分方程求解来引导平滑概率流。

Result: 与现有自回归生成框架相比，SeamFlow 通过边标记化提高了拓扑感知能力，同时消除了三维空间投影误差和人为的序列顺序偏差。大量实验表明其实现了出色的语义连贯性和极低的参数化畸变。

Conclusion: SeamFlow 有效弥补了传统几何优化与自回归生成方法各自的不足，在三维表面切割中同时取得了优异的语义连贯性与低参数化畸变，为该领域提供了新的生成式解决方案。

Abstract: 3D surface cutting and UV unwrapping are fundamental problems in computer graphics. Traditional geometric optimization methods mainly focus on reducing parameterization distortion, but they often overlook visual semantic coherence in seam layouts. Recent autoregressive generative methods improve semantic coherence, yet limited perception of mesh topology often causes inaccurate local cuts. To address these limitations, we introduce SeamFlow, a novel generative framework for 3D surface cutting. We reformulate the discrete mesh-cutting problem as continuous flow matching in a high-dimensional edge-probability space. Through continuous relaxation, SeamFlow learns a deterministic mapping from a Gaussian prior to a target seam-probability distribution. An evolution network couples local topological tokens with global shape priors and guides smooth probability flow through Ordinary Differential Equation solving. Compared with existing autoregressive generative frameworks, SeamFlow improves topology awareness through edge tokenization while eliminating both 3D spatial projection errors and artificial sequential-order bias. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SeamFlow achieves exceptional semantic coherence and remarkably low parameterization distortion. The project page is https://meshy-dev.github.io/seamflow.

</details>


### [30] [LUMIN: Lightweight Universal Manufacturing Inspection Network for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04775)
*Pengfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对工业异常检测的两大工程瓶颈（记忆库构建延迟与推理效率），提出PSP（插件采样器流水线）——一种基于18维像素元数据和五种互补视觉插件的四阶段自适应记忆库采样流水线，可在零骨干网络前向传播下完成采样，构建速度比FPS快341倍。同时提出两项工程优化策略：并行记忆库相似度计算（推理内存与延迟降低超95%）与分层像素采样（评估时间降低20倍）。并以LUMIN（轻量级通用制造检测网络）为验证载体，在五个基准上证明PSP以近乎随机构建成本匹配最先进采样精度，推理优化以极小精度损失大幅缩短评估时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测在工程部署上面临两个瓶颈：传统采样算法（如FPS、K-Means）依赖大量骨干网络前向传播和迭代距离计算，记忆库构建耗时从几分钟到几小时不等；多尺度特征提取等重计算组件难以满足产线毫秒级实时性要求。因此亟需针对采样效率和推理优化的工业部署方案。

Method: 提出两条核心技术路线：(1) PSP（插件采样器流水线）：一种四阶段自适应记忆库采样流水线，基于18维像素元数据与五种互补视觉插件，零骨干前向传播完成采样；粗筛选为亚秒级数值排序，元数据提取为一次性离线成本，支持渐进式部署与增量更新。(2) 两项工程优化策略：并行记忆库相似度计算（降低推理内存与延迟超95%）与分层像素采样用于大规模评估（计算时间降低20倍且指标稳定）。以极端分割头压缩的LUMIN网络作为验证载体，系统探索精度-效率前沿。

Result: 在五个基准上的实验表明：PSP以近乎随机的构建成本（比FPS快341倍）匹配了最先进的采样精度；推理优化将评估时间降低20倍，精度损失可忽略不计；并行记忆库相似度计算使推理内存与延迟降低超95%，分层采样使计算时间降低20倍而指标保持稳定。

Conclusion: 本文证明工业异常检测可以在近乎零骨干前向传播的成本下实现最先进采样精度，并通过并行计算与分层采样等工程优化大幅提升推理效率，为产线级毫秒级实时异常检测的工业部署提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection faces two engineering bottlenecks: memory bank construction latency and inference efficiency. Traditional sampling algorithms (Farthest Point Sampling, K-Means, etc.) rely on numerous backbone forward passes and iterative distance computations, with construction times ranging from minutes to hours; heavy computation components such as multi-scale feature extraction struggle to meet the millisecond-level real-time requirements of production lines. This paper focuses on sampling efficiency and inference optimization for industrial deployment with two core contributions: (1) PSP (Plugin Sampler Pipeline)---a four-stage adaptive memory bank sampling pipeline based on 18-dimensional pixel metadata and five complementary visual plugins. PSP completes all sampling with zero backbone forward passes; coarse filtering is sub-second numerical sorting, and metadata extraction is a one-time offline cost. PSP supports progressive deployment and incremental updates. (2) Two engineering optimization strategies---parallel memory bank similarity computation (reducing inference memory and latency by over 95\%) and stratified pixel sampling for large-scale evaluation (reducing computation time by 20$\times$ while keeping metrics stable). As a vehicle for validation, we introduce LUMIN (Lightweight Universal Manufacturing Inspection Network) with extreme segmentation-head compression, systematically exploring the accuracy-efficiency frontier against strong baselines. Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate that PSP matches state-of-the-art sampling accuracy at near-random construction cost (341$\times$ faster than FPS), while inference optimizations reduce evaluation time by 20$\times$ with negligible accuracy loss.

</details>


### [31] [CoMLP: Cooperatively-Gated MLPs for Fine-Grained Cross-Modal Information Fusion in Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04781)
*Mingyuan Meng,Shuchang Ye,Mingjian Li,Zhenyu Zhao,Jinman Kim,Lei Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoMLP，一种基于协同门控MLP的细粒度跨模态信息融合模块，用于医学图像分割。CoMLP通过互补的区域和扩张MLP交互实现协同交叉门控，同时捕获局部与全局跨模态依赖，并在多源融合架构中完成图像间融合（多模态影像）和视觉-语言融合（影像与临床报告），无需依赖密集交叉注意力。在五个医学分割基准（涵盖2D/3D、临床报告、多模态和多样解剖区域）上取得一致提升，消融实验证明高分辨率细粒度交互和局部-全局互补融合是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态融合方法多依赖交叉注意力，其计算量随空间分辨率快速增加，导致高分辨率特征图（特别是体数据）上难以进行密集的跨模态交互，无法充分保留细微空间细节并捕获语义依赖。

Method: 提出CoMLP协同门控MLP模块：基于互补的区域MLP与扩张MLP交互实现协同交叉门控，同时建模局部和全局跨模态依赖；构建多源融合架构，CoMLP既执行跨成像模态的图像间融合，又执行视觉特征与文本报告的视觉-语言融合，完全避开密集交叉注意力。

Result: 在覆盖2D/3D图像、临床报告、多成像模态和多种解剖区域的五个医学分割基准上，相比最先进的多模态和语言引导分割方法取得一致提升；消融实验表明高空间分辨率的细粒度交互以及互补的局部-全局融合是性能增益的关键。

Conclusion: 基于MLP的交互可以作为医学图像分割中细粒度跨模态信息融合的有效替代方案，在保持计算效率的同时实现高质量融合。

Abstract: Multi-modal medical images and clinical reports provide complementary anatomical, functional, and semantic information for medical image segmentation. Effectively exploiting these heterogeneous sources requires fine-grained cross-modal information fusion that preserves subtle spatial details while capturing semantic dependencies across modalities. Existing fusion approaches frequently rely on cross-attention, whose computational burden increases rapidly with spatial resolution, making dense cross-modal interaction difficult on high-resolution feature maps, particularly for volumetric medical images. In this work, we propose CoMLP, a cooperatively-gated MLP module for fine-grained cross-modal information fusion in medical image segmentation. CoMLP models cross-modal dependencies through cooperative cross-gating, built upon complementary regional and dilated MLP interactions, to capture local and global cross-modal dependencies. We further develop a multi-source fusion architecture in which CoMLP performs both inter-image fusion across imaging modalities and vision-language fusion between visual features and textual reports, enabling heterogeneous information to be integrated without relying on dense cross-attention. Extensive experiments on five medical segmentation benchmarks, covering 2D/3D images, clinical reports, multiple imaging modalities, and diverse anatomical regions, demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art multi-modal and language-guided segmentation methods. Ablation studies further show that fine-grained interaction at high spatial resolutions and complementary local-global fusion are critical to the performance gains. These results demonstrate the potential of MLP-based interaction as an effective alternative for fine-grained cross-modal information fusion in medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [32] [CLON: Cue-Calibrated Linguistic Object Onboarding for Zero-Shot 6D Pose Front-Ends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04784)
*Seojin Ji,Yoojin Kwon,Hyung-Sin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CLON（Cue-Calibrated Linguistic Object Onboarding），一种无需针对新物体进行任务特定训练的前端模块。它通过构建语言语义记忆引导SAM 3生成高召回率候选框，并利用一次性计算的物体集线索权重进行校准评分，在七个BOP-Classic-Core数据集上较CNOS和SAM-6D前端在检测AP、分割AP和下游6D位姿AR上分别提升8.1、6.2和最高4.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本6D位姿估计依赖强大的下游位姿求解器，但其性能常受前端限制：物体提议必须保留部分可见的真阳性候选，同时拒绝语义上看似合理的干扰项。现有前端难以平衡召回率与精确率，因此需要一种无需任务特定训练即可高效生成与筛选候选框的方案。

Method: CLON主要包括两部分：（1）为在册物体集构建语言语义记忆，用于自顶向下地引导SAM 3生成高召回率的候选框；（2）在场景推理前一次性从物体集计算"线索权重"，并在在线评分阶段保持固定，用于校准候选框评分，以抑制语义合理但错误的干扰项。

Result: 在七个BOP-Classic-Core数据集上，相较于CNOS和SAM-6D前端，CLON在检测AP上平均提升8.1个百分点，在分割AP上提升6.2个百分点，在下游6D位姿AR上最高提升4.1个百分点。

Conclusion: CLON通过语言语义记忆与线索权重校准机制有效解决了零样本前端中召回率与精确率的权衡问题，显著增强了候选框的提议质量，并进而提升了整体6D位姿估计的性能，且无需为新物体进行任何训练。

Abstract: Zero-shot 6D pose estimation pipelines increasingly rely on strong downstream pose solvers, but their performance is often limited by the front-end: object proposals must preserve partially visible true positives while rejecting semantically plausible distractors. We introduce Cue-Calibrated Linguistic Object Onboarding (CLON), a front-end requiring no task-specific training for new objects. Given rendered templates of the onboarded object set, CLON constructs a linguistic semantic memory for top-down proposal generation and object-set cue weights for calibrated proposal scoring. The linguistic memory guides SAM 3 toward high-recall proposals for onboarded objects, while cue weights are computed once from the onboarded object set before scene inference and kept fixed during online scoring. On seven BOP-Classic-Core datasets, CLON improves detection AP by 8.1 percentage points (pp), segmentation AP by 6.2 pp, and downstream 6D pose AR by up to 4.1 pp over CNOS and SAM-6D front-ends.

</details>


### [33] [An Attention-Guided Global and Local Fusion Framework for Lesion-Focused Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04791)
*Mst Shafia Tasnima,Md Samaun Elaheea,Tanjim Taharat Aurpab,Md Musfique Anwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于DenseNet-121的三分支注意力引导深度学习框架，通过自适应融合全局上下文信息与病灶局部信息来提升病灶聚焦图像分类性能。该框架包含全局分支（生成Grad-CAM注意力图）、CBAM增强的局部分支（提取聚焦区域的精细特征）以及自适应融合分支（学习实例特定权重）。在合成斑块数据集及皮肤病变、番石榴叶、葡萄叶三个基准数据集上，融合分支均优于单独使用全局或局部分支，在皮肤病变数据集上达到97.75%准确率，番石榴叶数据集上达到99.64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
