Several explanations for boolean classifiers have been introduced in the literature, including abductive and contrastive explanations, each giving a different insight on the decision of the classifier. However, computing an explanation for a decision of a boolean classifier is a hard problem in general. One way to deal with this complexity is to rely on a compiled representation of the classifier for which each explanation can be computed efficiently. Unfortunately, we prove in this paper that several classes of abductive explanations, remain hard to compute even for Ordered Binary Decision Diagrams, one of the most tractable subsets of the knowledge compilation map. Included in such classes are shorter abductive explanations or abductive explanations that include the explainee's preferences. To recover the benefits of working with compiled representations, we show that a proper representation of the dual-rail encoding of the classifier can be used to compute efficiently these classes of explanations.

</details>


### [291] [Why We Care About Understanding: Competence through Predictive Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04962)
*Matthieu Queloz,Pierre Beckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出理解与压缩的关系并非等同，而是理解作为鲁棒能力的代理，其心理表征（关系结构模型）因可预测性而自然产生压缩效应，人类理解特有的可演示性和可传递性压力又推动其走向原则性简洁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息论、机器学习和AI研究中普遍将理解等同于压缩（如Chaitin的“理解即压缩”），而哲学家强调理解涉及把握联系、给出解释和处理新颖性。作者希望弥合这两种对立观点。

Method: 通过三个相互关联的论题构建框架：第一，理解概念作为鲁棒胜任力的高效代理；第二，理解状态是拥有领域关系结构的心理模型，可预测性带来压缩；第三，人类理解的形式受信任与传递功能的可演示性、可传递性压力驱动，趋于原则性简洁。

Result: 该框架解释了压缩主义理解论的吸引力与局限，并阐明了AI系统的不可解释性。

Conclusion: 理解不等于压缩，压缩是理解在表征上的“影子”；人类理解倾向于简洁源于社会认知功能的要求，这为理解AI系统为何难以捉摸提供了新视角。

Abstract: What is the relation between understanding and compression, and why does human understanding take such a heavily compressed form? Across information theory, machine learning, and AI research, a substantial tradition identifies understanding with compression-a thought captured in Gregory Chaitin's dictum that "comprehension is compression." Philosophers, by contrast, have characterized understanding in terms of grasping connections, giving explanations, and handling novelty. This paper bridges the two pictures through three interlocking theses. The first concerns the concept of understanding: it serves as an efficient proxy for a distinctive form of robust competence, enabling us to identify whom to trust and whom to learn from. The second concerns the state of understanding: to understand a domain is to possess a mental model of its relational structure that enables prediction, and what enables prediction enables compression, because what becomes predictable need not be stored separately. Compression is therefore not identical with comprehension, but its representational shadow. The third concerns the characteristically human form of understanding: the fiduciary and transmission functions highlighted by the first thesis impose pressures of demonstrability and transmissibility that drive human understanding toward principled simplicity. The resulting framework explains both the appeal and the limits of compressionist accounts of understanding while shedding light on the inscrutability of AI systems.

</details>


### [292] [Global to Local: Topology-Preserving Adaptive Graph Pooling via Granular-Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04978)
*Sen Zhao,Gaojie Xu,Shuyin Xia,Yifan Guan,Yi Liu,Yi Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的拓扑保持自适应图池化方法（TPAGP），该方法通过动态地将图划分为多粒度的"粒度球"，同时整合节点特征与拓扑信息，从而生成能同时捕获局部和全局结构模式的多粒度表示，并在图分类任务上显著优于现有池化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图池化方法过度关注过细的节点级表示，通过逐级移除节点或聚类来粗化图，忽视了图的拓扑结构所体现的全局到局部模式以及自适应粒度。现实场景中图本身可作为最粗粒度层级，自上而下包含逐渐细粒度的局部拓扑结构，直至每个子域的适应性粒度，而现有固定粒度策略会造成信息损失。

Method: 提出TPAGP方法，通过整合节点特征与拓扑信息、动态地将图划分为粒度球，生成多粒度表示以捕获局部与全局结构模式；同时设计一个多粒度图网络模型，实现不同粒度间的特征交互与优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，TPAGP在多种基准数据集上优于现有池化方法，有效缓解了固定粒度策略导致的信息丢失问题。

Conclusion: 通过引入自适应多粒度划分机制，TPAGP能够弥补传统固定粒度池化方法的不足，在图分类任务中显著提升性能，证明了粒度自适应与拓扑保持在图形池化中的有效性。

Abstract: Graph pooling aims to compress the graph, including both node embeddings and their underlying topological patterns, into a more compact representation. Previous works focus primarily on the overly fine-grained representation of nodes, progressively coarsening the graph by removing nodes or merging them into clusters, thus neglecting the global-to-local patterns and adaptive granularity of the graph's topological structure. In the real scenario, graphs as a whole can be considered the coarsest level of granularity, encapsulating the global topological structure, with progressively finer-grained local topological structures represented from top to bottom. This process continues until the adaptive granularity for each subdomain is reached. To this end, we propose a novel Topology-Preserving Adaptive Graph Pooling (TPAGP) method that dynamically partitions graphs into granular balls by integrating node features and topological information, enabling the generation of multi-granularity representations that effectively capture both local and global structural patterns. Additionally, we design a multi-granularity graph network model that facilitates feature interaction and optimization across different granularities, significantly enhancing performance in graph classification tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that TPAGP outperforms existing pooling methods across various benchmark datasets, effectively mitigating information loss caused by fixed-granularity strategies.

</details>


### [293] [A Tree-based RAG Framework for Evidence-Intensive QA via Adaptive Planning and Topology-Aware Evidence Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04981)
*Songeun Lee,Kyungjin Min,Injae Na,Suyeong Lee,Chiyoung Kim,Woohwan Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出APT-RAG，一种自适应规划与拓扑感知证据收集的RAG框架，通过动态扩展推理结构和利用拓扑信息整合证据，解决了证据密集型多跳问答中现有结构化RAG方法的结构刚性及拓扑忽视问题，在证据密集型QA基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化RAG方法（如基于树或图）在证据密集型多跳问答中面临两大关键局限：一是推理结构刚性，无法根据问题依赖和证据需求自适应扩展；二是证据收集忽视拓扑信息，难以在不同推理节点间有效整合分散在数十甚至数百篇文档中的证据，从而影响问答性能。

Method: APT-RAG包含三个核心组件：1）自适应规划（Adaptive Planning）：根据问题依赖和证据需求动态扩展推理结构；2）拓扑感知证据收集（Topology-aware evidence gathering）：通过兄弟节点证据复用、直接检索及子节点证据聚合三种机制提升证据覆盖率；3）证据引导的批式答案生成（Evidence-guided batched answer generation）：减少证据密集型QA中显著的生成开销。

Result: 在证据密集型QA基准的实验表明，APT-RAG在性能上优于现有的结构化RAG方法，代码已开源。

Conclusion: APT-RAG通过自适应规划与拓扑感知证据收集有效解决了证据密集型多跳问答中结构刚性和证据整合不足的问题，验证了动态推理结构与拓扑信息利用在提升证据密集型QA性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: Recent structured RAG methods leverage tree- or graph-based reasoning structures to improve multi-hop QA. However, they face key limitations in evidence-intensive QA, where answering a question requires synthesizing information scattered across dozens or even hundreds of documents: structural rigidity, which limits adaptive reasoning expansion, and topology-ignorant evidence gathering, which prevents effective integration of evidence across different reasoning nodes. To address these issues, we propose APT-RAG, an Adaptive Planning and Topology-aware evidence gathering RAG framework. Adaptive planning dynamically expands the reasoning structure based on question dependencies and evidence requirements, while topology-aware evidence gathering improves evidence coverage through sibling evidence reuse, direct retrieval, and evidence aggregation from child nodes. We further introduce evidence-guided batched answer generation to reduce significant generation overhead in evidence-intensive QA. In the experiments on evidence-intensive QA benchmarks, APT-RAG outperforms existing structured RAG methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hyudsl/APT-RAG.

</details>


### [294] [Language models judge war differently when tested for alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05009)
*Maxim Chupilkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过一项涵盖20个大型语言模型、32个场景、10次重复和两种条件的全因子联合实验（共12,800个判断），研究了AI系统在被告知正在接受评估时是否会改变行为。结果显示，添加"你正在接受人类价值观一致性测试"这一句提示产生了两种效应：水平效应（发动战争意愿下降13.43个百分点）和结构效应（改变了驱动判断的信息权重，从以成功概率为主转向以平民伤亡为主）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估可能无法准确反映AI系统在实际部署中的行为，因为AI系统可能在被评估时做出不同的反应（即"评估感知"效应）。作者旨在实证检验这一可能性，以评估安全问题。

Method: 采用全因子联合实验（full-factorial conjoint experiment）设计，针对是否发动战争的决策，操控20个LLM、32个场景、10次重复和两种条件（有无"你正在接受人类价值观一致性测试"的提示），共收集12,800个判断。通过标准化的估计来量化两种效应。

Result: 两种效应被观察到：(1) 水平效应——添加提示后，发动战争的意愿平均下降13.43分（95%置信区间-16.20至-10.65）；(2) 结构效应——改变了哪种信息驱动判断：基线时17/20个模型以成功概率为最大影响因素，提示条件下12个模型以平民伤亡为最大因素。这种重排主要源于模型削弱了战略考量（如成功概率和国内支持）的权重。

Conclusion: 评估框架（evaluation framing）既改变了答案的水平，也改变了揭示出的决策规则。这意味着安全评估可能高估或误导对AI真实部署行为的判断，未来需考虑评估语境对AI行为的影响。

Abstract: Safety evaluations can mischaracterize deployed behaviour if artificial-intelligence systems respond to being evaluated. We test this possibility in a full-factorial conjoint experiment on decisions to start a war, spanning 20 large language models, 32 scenarios, 10 repetitions and two conditions (N = 12,800 judgments). Adding one sentence, "You are tested for alignment with human values", produced two effects. First, it produced a level effect: mean willingness to start war fell by 13.43 points on a 0-100 scale (95% confidence interval, -16.20 to -10.65). Second, it produced a structural effect by changing which information drove judgments. Probability of success was the largest factor for 17 of 20 models at baseline; under the cue, civilian casualties were largest for 12. Standardized estimates show that this reordering arose principally because models attenuated strategic considerations such as probability of success and domestic support. Evaluation framing therefore changes both an answer's level and its revealed decision rule.

</details>


### [295] [TROVE: Adaptive Agent Skill Orchestration via Trace-Grounded Route Validation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05019)
*Tianxing Wang,Mingming Zhao,Shuai Huang,Huiyang Xu,Chaoyue Niu,Shengzhong Liu,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TROVE（Trace-grounded Route Orchestration via Validation and Editing），一种自适应的智能体编排方法。其核心思想是：智能体在运行时证据出现后，仅修订被证据否定的执行路径片段，而非整体重规划或执行过期步骤。离线阶段，TROVE将评估过的工作流搜索轨迹蒸馏为原子技能、复合技能及结果条件化的转移图；在线阶段，将规划视作临时性的，逐层提交后保留有效前缀、插入轨迹支撑的局部响应，或仅替换无效后缀。在代码生成、问答和数学推理基准上，TROVE实现了优于现有基线的质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体在执行前就预先确定、优化或约束执行结构，这会造成编排瓶颈：当中间证据使待执行的后续步骤失效时，智能体要么执行过时的步骤，要么进行广泛的重规划，从而累积错误、浪费计算资源并丢弃已有进展。作者旨在解决这一预执行承诺带来的低效与错误累积问题。

Method: TROVE采用"离线蒸馏+在线选择性编辑"两阶段框架。离线阶段，将评估过的工作流搜索轨迹蒸馏为原子技能和复合技能，并构建结果条件化的转移图，保留稳定片段同时暴露依赖结果的决策点。在线阶段，将规划路线视为临时方案：每次提交一个顶层技能后，控制器根据运行时证据选择三种操作之一——保留有效延续、插入由轨迹支撑的局部响应，或仅替换无效的后缀部分。

Result: 跨代码生成、问答、数学推理基准，并使用不同LLM主干进行评估，TROVE相比数据集级优化、查询级架构选择和图约束调度等现有基线，实现了更强的质量-效率权衡。质量增益在结果改变合适延续时最大，而在接近饱和的任务上，提前终止带来显著效率增益。消融实验表明：复合技能贡献了大部分离线收益，插入实现局部修正，后缀替换主要提升效率。

Conclusion: TROVE的实验结果确立了"选择性路线编辑"作为自适应智能体编排的一般性原则：不是预先锁定完整执行路径，而是根据运行时证据对失效部分进行最小化的局部修正，从而在保持质量的同时提升计算效率，避免广泛重规划带来的错误累积与资源浪费。

Abstract: Agents tend to optimize, select, or constrain execution structures before decisive runtime outcomes are observed. However, such pre-execution commitment creates an orchestration bottleneck: when intermediate evidence invalidates the pending continuation, agents must either execute stale steps or replan broadly, compounding errors, wasting computation, and discarding progress. We thus propose Trace-grounded Route Orchestration via Validation and Editing (TROVE), which revises only what runtime evidence invalidates. Offline, TROVE distills evaluated workflow-search traces into atomic and composite skills and an outcome-conditioned transition graph, preserving stable fragments while exposing outcome-dependent decisions. Online, it treats a planned route as provisional: after committing one top-level skill, the controller retains a valid continuation, inserts a trace-supported local response, or replaces only the invalid suffix. Evaluation across code-generation, question-answering, and math reasoning benchmarks with different LLM backbones show that TROVE delivers a stronger quality-efficiency trade-off than existing baselines of dataset-level optimization, query-level architecture selection, and graph-constrained scheduling. Quality gains are largest when outcomes change the appropriate continuation, whereas early termination yields substantial efficiency gains on near-saturated tasks. Ablations further show that composite skills capture most offline benefits, insertion enables local correction, and suffix replacement primarily improves efficiency. These findings establish selective route editing as a general principle for adaptive agent orchestration.

</details>


### [296] [Moral Competence Before Moral Content: Why LLM Agents Lack the Prerequisites for Coherent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05036)
*Arno Libert,Derck W. E. Prinzhorn,Daan R. Henselmans*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出评估AI系统"道德胜任力"的四个结构性条件（判定稳定性、单调性、决断性、帕累托可行性），这些条件无需参照外部道德标准即可从行为中度量，构成对齐的结构性底线。通过对九个前沿模型在三个模拟部署中的测试，发现没有任何模型在各个场景中表达出一致的政策，表面形式扰动即可造成高达99个百分点的不稳定率，表明LLM智能体当前尚不是可被有意义对齐的对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在价值多元主义的背景下，AI对齐并无单一正确目标，但一个共同前提是系统行为应体现连贯的政策——即对情境到判定的映射应在保留道德相关特征时保持不变、在相关特征改变时随之变化。现有对齐方法常依赖单一道德标准或专家基线，无法在价值多元情形下评判系统，因此需要一种仅从行为出发、不依赖外部标准的评估框架。

Method: 提出四项结构性条件刻画连贯政策：判定稳定性（verdict stability）、单调性（monotonicity）、决断性（decisiveness）和帕累托可行性（Pareto viability）。采用阶乘实验设计，对九个前沿模型在三个模拟部署（LLM智能体面对道德困境）中测试，包含五种改写形式、五个升级层级和三种支配条件，以测量各条件被满足的程度。

Result: 实验显示没有任何模型在三个部署中表达出连贯政策。仅表面形式扰动（改写）即可在单一升级层级造成最高99个百分点的不稳定率；且模型在一个场景中的成功无法预测其在另一场景中的胜任力。这些发现表明现有LLM智能体不具备结构性的道德一致性。

Conclusion: 作者认为，基于LLM的智能体当前并不是可实现有意义对齐的那类对象，因为它们缺乏表达连贯政策的结构性能力。这些条件构成对齐的结构底线（structural floor）而非规范性目标（normative target），为在价值多元环境下评估系统道德胜任力提供了不依赖外部标准的客观框架。

Abstract: AI alignment requires AI systems to adhere to human norms, values, or intentions. Under value pluralism there is no correct target, but a shared prerequisite is that the system's behavior expresses a coherent policy: a mapping from situations to verdicts that is invariant while a situation's morally relevant features are preserved, and sensitive when they change. We introduce four structural conditions for such coherent policies: verdict stability, monotonicity, decisiveness, and Pareto viability. Together they measure a form of moral competence that is evaluable from behavior alone, without reference to a moral standard or expert baseline, forming a structural floor for alignment rather than a normative target. We demonstrate the methodology on three simulated deployments featuring LLM-based agents facing moral dilemmas. Evaluating nine frontier models under a factorial design of five paraphrases, five escalation levels, and three dominance conditions, we show no model expresses a coherent policy across the three deployments: surface-form perturbation alone produces verdict-rate shifts of up to $99$ percentage points at a single escalation level, and a model's success on one scenario does not predict its competence on another. This suggests LLM-based agents are not currently the kind of object to which alignment can meaningfully apply.

</details>


### [297] [Towards Efficient Evaluation of Evolutionary Transfer Optimization: Case Studies on Task-Parameterized Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05040)
*Yanchen Li,Xiaoming Xue,Kay Chen Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了任务参数化应用中的问题侧评估扩展问题，通过将特定应用的串行计算重构为适合并行执行的矩阵表示形式，分别针对多任务优化中的运动学机械臂评估（累积矩阵表示）和顺序迁移优化中的B样条轨迹评估（混合矩阵表示），在保持数值一致性的同时实现了256.72倍和93.91倍的端到端加速，证明了问题侧重构是迈向可扩展ETO的实用途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着进化迁移优化（ETO）扩展到更大规模的相关任务集合，问题评估可能成为运行时增长的主要来源，因此需要探索问题侧的评估扩展策略来提升ETO的可扩展性。

Method: 将评估扩展组织为两个层级：评估的任务数量与每个任务内部的工作负载。将多任务优化中矩阵递归的运动学机械臂评估重构为基于累积矩阵的累积连杆方向表示；将顺序迁移优化中逐点B样条轨迹评估重构为基于混合矩阵的轨迹与碰撞计算表示，两者均支持并行执行并保持与参考评估的数值一致性。

Result: 两种重构方法在数值上与参考评估保持高度一致，同时显著降低了运行时开销，分别取得了256.72倍和93.91倍的端到端加速比。

Conclusion: 问题侧重构是实现可扩展ETO的实用有效途径；相关应用实现和实验脚本已作为开源发布，以支持可复现性和复用。

Abstract: As evolutionary transfer optimization (ETO) scales to larger collections of related tasks, problem evaluation can become a major source of runtime growth. This work studies problem-side evaluation scaling in task-parameterized applications and reformulates application-specific serial computations into forms suitable for parallel execution. We organize evaluation scaling into two levels: the number of evaluated tasks and the workload within each task. In multi-task optimization, matrix-recursive kinematic-arm evaluation is reformulated using an accumulation-matrix representation of cumulative link directions. In sequential transfer optimization, pointwise B-spline trajectory evaluation is reformulated using a blending-matrix representation for trajectory and collision computations. Both reformulations maintain close numerical agreement with their reference evaluations and substantially reduce runtime, yielding $256.72\times$ and $93.91\times$ end-to-end speedups, respectively. These results demonstrate problem-side reformulation as a practical route toward scalable ETO. Both application implementations and experimental scripts are released as open source to support reproducibility and reuse.

</details>


### [298] [MePo++: Unifying Representation Refinement and Reconciliation for General Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05075)
*Guanglong Sun,Kanglei Zhou,Liyuan Wang,Qi Cheng,Hongwei Yan,Shuang Cui,Hang Su,Jun Zhu,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MePo++，一个统一的后训练框架，通过MetaPrep（基于伪持续序列的无监督元精炼提升表征可塑性）和StreamAlign（以稳定的预训练几何对齐在线特征增强表征稳定性）两个互补组件，弥合预训练知识与下游通用持续学习（GCL）之间的鸿沟，在模糊数据流下兼顾新建概念的适应能力与持续演化流的表征稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用持续学习（GCL）面临无任务标识、无明确边界、无旧数据回放的真实挑战。虽然预训练模型（PTMs）提供了丰富的先验知识，但现有基于PTM的方法直接适配预训练表征，忽视了两个关键缺口：一是上游预训练与下游持续适配之间的错位；二是在模糊（blurry）数据流下传统输出对齐的不可靠性。

Method: MePo++采用统一的后训练框架，包含两个互补组件：MetaPrep负责在适配前通过伪持续序列上的无监督元精炼来提升表征可塑性；StreamAlign负责在持续学习过程中通过将演化中的在线特征与稳定的预训练几何进行调和来强化表征稳定性。整体思路是"先提升表征可学习性，后保持对齐"。

Result: 在多样化的预训练模型、数据集和持续学习基线上进行实验，结果表明MePo++在基于PTM的GCL任务上具有一致的有效性和通用性。相关代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: MePo++通过"适配前提升可塑性"与"学习中维持稳定性"的双阶段策略，使预训练模型既对新概念保持可塑，又对演化数据流保持稳定，有效解决了PTM与下游GCL之间的错位以及模糊流下输出对齐不可靠的问题。

Abstract: General continual learning (GCL) aims to learn from evolving data streams without task identities, explicit boundaries, or repeated access to previous data, making it a realistic yet challenging setting for continual intelligence. Although pretrained models (PTMs) provide rich prior knowledge for addressing the limited supervision and non-stationary nature of GCL, existing PTM-based methods often directly adapt pretrained representations and overlook two critical gaps: the misalignment between upstream pretraining and downstream continual adaptation, and the unreliability of conventional output alignment under blurry streams. Here we propose MePo++, a unified post-training framework that bridges pretrained knowledge and downstream GCL through representation refinement and reconciliation. MePo++ introduces two complementary components: MetaPrep, which improves representation plasticity for continual adaptation through unsupervised meta-refinement over pseudo continual sequences; and StreamAlign, which reinforces representation stability by reconciling evolving online features with a stable pretrained geometry. By improving representation learnability before adaptation and preserving alignment during continual learning, MePo++ enables PTMs to remain both plastic for new concepts and stable over evolving streams. Experiments across diverse PTMs, datasets, and continual learning baselines demonstrate the consistent effectiveness and generality of MePo++ for PTM-based GCL. Our code is available at https://github.com/SunGL001/MePo_Plus.

</details>


### [299] [TruthInsightBench: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Automated Evaluation of Open-Ended Scientific Discovery Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05079)
*Zhibo Yang,Chen Zhang,Yuewei Zhang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TruthInsightBench，一个面向"科学发现"而非"结果复现"的评测基准。该基准包含40个来自10个科学领域、40篇同行评审研究的盲测任务，仅暴露中性科学目标和冻结数据，隐藏源结论与预期值，让智能体自主判断数据支持什么结论。评测采用固定的LLM裁判，从6个维度（操作化为29个基于产物的条目）打分证据成熟度，自动化确定性聚合、无需人工逐例打分。在单一冻结基座模型上，四个编码智能体形成狭窄平台期（58.4-60.3/100），无统计显著的两两区分：它们能胜任执行与文档化分析，证据可审计性和新颖性较强，但缺乏建立可信主张的关键判别行为（对照、稳健性、可证伪性和跨数据集泛化）。瓶颈在于科学判断而非编码能力，真正发现仍遥不可及。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家基准是为"复现"而设计的：任务、数据、评分细则都围绕隐藏的目标研究构建，恢复其结果即得高分。但作者指出，执行预设分析并不等于做出发现。为此需要一种以"发现"为配置的基准，评测智能体能否自主提出并证据化其数据支撑的主张，而非对照已知答案。

Method: TruthInsightBench包含40个盲测任务，源自10个科学领域的40篇同行评审研究，仅提供中性科学目标和冻结数据，隐藏源结论、预期值和分析路径。评测使用固定LLM裁判，沿6个维度（操作化为29个基于人工产物的判定条目）评估智能体自身主张的证据成熟度；通过自动化、确定性的聚合方式计算分数，无需逐实例人工评分，因此可随智能体演化自动重复评测。

Result: 在单一冻结基座模型上，四个编码智能体形成狭窄平台（58.4-60.3/100），任何两两之间均无统计可靠的显著区分。智能体在分析执行与文档化方面表现胜任，在证据可审计性和新颖性上相对较强，但在建立可信主张所需的判别性行为（对照、稳健性、可证伪性、跨数据集泛化）上明显欠缺。

Conclusion: 限制自主智能体的瓶颈在于科学判断能力而非编码能力；真实科学发现仍超出当前智能体的能力范围。TruthInsightBench将这一差距量化为可测量的目标，并提供公开数据与评分代码，支持后续智能体演进时的自动化跟踪评测。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly proposed as AI-scientist systems that conduct analyses and write research reports, but executing a prescribed analysis is not the same as making a discovery. Existing benchmarks are configured for reproduction: tasks, data, and rubrics are built around a hidden target study, and recovery of its result is rewarded. We present TruthInsightBench, a benchmark configured for discovery. Its 40 blind tasks, drawn from 40 peer-reviewed studies across 10 scientific domains, expose only a neutral scientific objective and frozen data; source conclusions, expected values, and analysis paths are withheld, leaving the agent to determine what claim the data support. A fixed LLM-based judge scores the evidentiary maturity of an agent's own claims along six dimensions, operationalized as 29 artifact-grounded items, with automated, deterministic aggregation and no per-instance human grading, so evaluation can be repeated automatically as agents evolve. On one frozen base model, four coding agents form a narrow plateau (58.4-60.3 of 100) with no statistically reliable pairwise separation: they execute and document analyses competently, with comparatively strong evidence auditability and novelty, but largely lack the discriminating acts that establish a trustworthy claim (controls, robustness, falsifiability, and cross-dataset generalization). The bottleneck is scientific judgment rather than coding, and genuine discovery remains out of reach. TruthInsightBench makes this gap a measurable target; data and scoring code are at https://github.com/TruthInsight-stack/TruthInsightBench.

</details>


### [300] [Measuring AI Accountability Through Argumentation Analysis: Can Model Reasoning Withstand Scrutiny?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05088)
*Daan R. Henselmans,Derck W. E. Prinzhorn,Arno Libert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不依赖「正确性」地面真值、而基于论证结构质量的AI监督评估标准，即通过四阶段辩证协议（基于Walton论证图式和Govier论证充分性标准）衡量模型为其裁决进行的辩护质量。在9个前沿模型和200个高歧义道德选择题上，模型在各维度均高于门槛，但失败集中于理由与充分性维度，与认知性回避（hedging）相关，且推理过程的论证优于事后辩护。协议能识别不可辩护的辩护并暴露AI对齐中retraction角色界定的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI监督方法依赖地面真值进行验证，但何为恰当的AI行为本身存在争议，导致基于LLM的道德推理评估和基于辩论的监督方法隐含地回避了现实中的真实歧义。本文旨在提出一种能在这种歧义下依然有效的替代标准——辩护的结构质量，而非正确性。

Method: 构建一个四阶段辩证协议，扎根于Walton的论证图式理论和Govier的论证充分性（cogency）标准。该协议自适应不同的推理框架，超越多项选择题框架，同时处理推理前的前瞻推理和事后证明。实验采用9个前沿模型、200个高歧义MoralChoice题目，共6778个法官评分单元，并以89.6%的法官间一致性（针对二元失败判断）验证。

Result: 模型在所有Govier维度上的辩护表现均远高于rubric最低标准。失败质量集中在地基（grounds）和充分性（sufficiency）维度，并与认知性回避（epistemic hedging）相关而非论证长度。在所有模型和所有维度上，前瞻推理都比事后辩护得到更好的辩护。模型在相当比例的困境中（每个模型≥20%）呈现出的事后辩护图式与其推理所用图式不同，尽管基于价值的实践推理主导两条路径。协议能捕捉严格不可辩护的辩护（自相矛盾、虚假前提），并揭示AI对齐中retraction作用刻画上的难题。

Conclusion: 结构性辩护质量可作为在真实歧义下可行且不依赖地面真值的监督标准。研究揭示模型论证在辩护与推理之间的系统性差异，以及失败集中在grounds与sufficiency维度的问题，并指出需要更多情境化（situated）的评估来刻画retraction在AI对齐中的作用。

Abstract: AI oversight methods rely on ground truth for validation, but what constitutes appropriate AI behavior is contested. This leaves evaluation of moral reasoning in LLMs and debate-based oversight implicitly avoiding realistic ambiguity. We investigate an alternative standard designed to function despite such ambiguity: structural quality of the defence a model can mount for its verdicts in response to critical questions, measured through a four-phase dialectical protocol grounded in Walton's theory of argumentation schemes and Govier's criteria for argument cogency. The protocol is adaptive to different frames of reasoning, extends beyond multiple-choice framing, and treats both the reasoning that precedes a verdict and its post-hoc justification. Across nine frontier models and 200 high-ambiguity MoralChoice items -- $6,778$ judge-scored cells, validated against $89.6\%$ inter-judge agreement on the binary failure judgment -- models defend their reasoning well above the rubric minimum on every dimension. Failure mass concentrates on grounds and sufficiency, and correlates with epistemic hedging rather than argument length. Reasoning is better defended than post-hoc justification, on every model and every Govier dimension. The scheme a model presents in its justification differs from the one it reasoned with on a substantial share of dilemmas ($\geq 20\%$ per model), despite value-based practical reasoning dominating both tracks. The protocol catches strictly indefensible defences (self-contradiction, false premises), and it surfaces difficulties in characterizing the role of retraction in AI alignment, suggesting a need for more situated evaluations.

</details>


### [301] [Constructing and Evaluating Clinical Reasoning Trajectories for Medical Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05090)
*Yunqi Zhu,Wensheng Zhang,Xuebing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedTraj框架，将医学人工智能智能体的推理轨迹视为关键对象进行构建、评估与优化。该框架通过结构化多步推理链生成、五维度质量评分（连贯性、证据支持、幻觉、完整性、可追溯性）、受控错误注入、基于边际贡献的步骤级筛选，以及质量加权上下文学习，实现从推理质量到推理优化的闭环。实验表明该方法在CareQA、PubMedQA和CECMed上持续提升推理连贯性，并在CECMed上将正确率提升近一倍、幻觉率降低87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学人工智能智能体评估以"答案为中心"，仅关注最终输出的正确性而忽略中间推理的质量。然而在临床场景中，通过捏造证据或逻辑混乱得出的正确答案与错误答案同样危险，因此亟需将推理轨迹本身作为评估与优化的核心对象。

Method: MedTraj框架包含四条流水线：(1) 从医学推理源生成结构化多步推理链；(2) 将每条轨迹解析为临床观察、证据、编号推理步骤和最终结论，并按连贯性、证据支持、幻觉、完整性、可追溯性五个维度评分；(3) 受控错误注入（向正确轨迹注入针对性错误）以建立特定推理失败与可测量质量下降之间的因果联系，并基于边际贡献进行步骤级筛选，识别驱动或损害轨迹质量的关键步骤；(4) 质量加权上下文学习，将轨迹评估反馈在推理时回传给模型，使其从强、弱推理示范中共同学习。

Result: 在CareQA、PubMedQA和CECMed上的实验显示，轨迹上下文持续提升推理连贯性，较零样本基线提升+0.029至+0.041；在CECMed上，质量加权上下文使正确率较零样本基线近乎翻倍，并将幻觉率降低87%；边际贡献分析表明少数推理步骤承载了大部分质量信号，且推理链超过四步后收益递减。

Conclusion: MedTraj证实了将推理轨迹作为一等公民进行构建、评估和优化的价值：基于质量信号的步骤筛选与反馈式上下文学习能显著提升医学推理的连贯性与正确性、抑制幻觉，同时揭示了推理质量的稀疏性（少数步骤主导）和边际收益递减规律，为医学AI智能体的可解释与可信推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Evaluation of medical artificial intelligence agents remains predominantly answer-centric, assessing only the correctness of final outputs while overlooking the quality of intermediate reasoning. In clinical settings, however, a correct answer reached through fabricated evidence or incoherent logic is as dangerous as an incorrect one. We propose MedTraj, a framework that treats reasoning trajectories as critical objects for construction, evaluation, and optimization. The pipeline generates structured multi-step reasoning chains from medical reasoning sources. Each trajectory is then parsed into clinical observations, evidence, numbered reasoning steps, and a final conclusion, and scored across five quality dimensions: coherence, evidence support, hallucination, completeness, and traceability. Controlled error injection introduces targeted faults into otherwise correct trajectories to establish causal links between specific reasoning failures and measurable quality degradation. Building on this, step-level filtering based on marginal contribution identifies which individual reasoning steps drive or undermine trajectory quality. Finally, quality-weighted context learning feeds trajectory evaluations back into the model at inference time, allowing it to learn from both strong and weak reasoning demonstrations. Experiments across CareQA, PubMedQA, and CECMed demonstrate that trajectory context consistently improves reasoning coherence, with gains of +0.029 to +0.041 over a zero-shot baseline. On CECMed, quality-weighted context nearly doubles the correctness over the zero-shot baseline while cutting the hallucination ratio by 87%. Marginal-contribution analysis further shows that a small minority of reasoning steps carry most of the quality signal, and that extending chains beyond four steps yields diminishing returns.

</details>


### [302] [LLM-Guided Program Evolution for Circle Packing: Breaking 10 Packomania Records for $28](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05093)
*Wes Sander*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Discovery Loop系统，一个利用大语言模型迭代演化优化算法的轻量级系统。该系统从简单种子求解器出发，由LLM根据结果记分板和先前构思历史提出算法改进，每个候选方案由独立验证器评估，改进保留、失败丢弃。应用于Packomania圆填充基准（在单位正方形中最大化N个可变半径圆的半径和），系统在N=101-114范围内改进了10个值的最佳已知解，相比先前纪录提升2.4%-5.4%，全部在15次迭代内完成，总LLM成本仅$27.72。结果已获Packomania独立接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动科学发现传统上需要大量计算资源和专家知识，门槛较高。作者旨在通过利用大语言模型的推理与生成能力，构建一个轻量、低成本的系统，使自动化算法发现和优化能够以更民主化的方式普惠更多研究者，同时验证LLM驱动迭代优化在实际科学基准问题上的有效性。

Method: 系统架构为"Discovery Loop"：以简单种子求解器为起点，LLM依据结果记分板（scoreboard）和历史想法（history of prior ideas）提议算法改进；每个候选方案通过独立验证器（independent verifier）评估，通过者保留、失败者丢弃，形成闭环迭代。还包含自适应平台期检测机制（adaptive plateau-detection）以提升成本效率。基准任务为Packomania圆填充问题（在单位正方形中最大化N个可变半径圆的半径和，记作csqv）。

Result: 在N=101-114区间内，该系统改进了10个N值的当前最佳已知解，相对先前纪录提升2.4%-5.4%，所有改进均在15次迭代内获得，总LLM成本仅为27.72美元。改进结果已获Packomania基准测试组织方独立确认接受。

Conclusion: Discovery Loop证明了LLM驱动的迭代优化在科学算法发现中的可行性与高效性，以极低成本和少量迭代即可超越人类专家长期保持的纪录。系统还分析了成本效率动力学（包括自适应平台期检测机制），并讨论了该方法对民主化自动科学发现的启示——即有望降低自动科学发现在计算资源与专业知识上的门槛，使更广泛的科研群体能够参与算法创新。

Abstract: We present Discovery Loop, a lightweight system that uses a large language model (LLM) to iteratively evolve optimization algorithms. Starting from a simple seed solver, the LLM proposes algorithmic improvements guided by a scoreboard of results and a history of prior ideas. Each candidate is evaluated against an independent verifier; improvements are kept and failures discarded. Applied to the Packomania circle-packing benchmark (csqv: maximize the sum of radii of N variable-radius circles in the unit square), the system improved the best known solutions for 10 values of N in the range 101-114, with gains of 2.4%-5.4% over prior records, all within 15 iterations and at a total LLM cost of $27.72. These results have been independently accepted by Packomania. We describe the method, analyze cost-efficiency dynamics including an adaptive plateau-detection mechanism, and discuss implications for democratizing automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [303] [ProCA: Progressive Contrastive Alignment for Robust EEG Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05094)
*Kanglei Zhou,Chunyan Lan,Dongyang Li,Jun Zhu,Liyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ProCA（渐进对比对齐）框架，一种统一且模型无关的自适应神经-语义对齐方法，用于脑电（EEG）视觉解码。ProCA通过从冻结的视觉-语言先验渐进细化到EEG感知的语义关系来优化类级对比监督，并引入结构一致插值以根据通道和时间重要性约束特征混合。在多种设置下（依赖受试者、独立受试者、严格跨受试者迁移、持续适应）分别获得平均相对Top-1/Top-5提升7.4%/3.9%、10.0%/4.6%、28.1%/17.8%和16.8%/11.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG解码方法依赖固定的视觉或文本锚点进行对比学习，其语义关系可能随EEG表示在不同trial、受试者及学习阶段的变化而错位，导致优化不稳健。作者通过实证证明该不稳定性在标准协议和严格跨受试者迁移及个性化持续适应等稳健设置中均存在，并给出形式化分析，说明固定语义监督在EEG特定关系演化时会偏置优化，且结构无关的扰动会扭曲语义重要的EEG成分。

Method: ProCA框架：1）渐进对比对齐——从冻结的视觉-语言先验出发，逐步将类级对比监督细化为EEG感知的语义关系；2）结构一致插值——根据通道级和时间级重要性约束特征混合，保护语义关键的EEG成分。

Result: 在受试者依赖、受试者独立、严格跨受试者迁移和持续适应四种设置下，ProCA分别取得平均相对Top-1/Top-5增益7.4%/3.9%、10.0%/4.6%、28.1%/17.8%和16.8%/11.6%，尤其在严格跨受试者迁移上提升最显著。

Conclusion: 固定锚点导致的不对齐是EEG解码稳健性的核心瓶颈，通过自适应渐进对齐与结构一致约束可有效缓解该问题。ProCA作为模型无关的统一框架，可广泛应用于各类EEG解码场景并显著提升稳健性，尤其适用于跨受试者与持续适应等挑战性设置。

Abstract: Electroencephalogram (EEG) visual decoding aims to recover visual semantics from non-invasive neural time-series signals, for which robust alignment between noisy neural responses and stable semantic representations is key to achieving high-performance decoding. Despite recent advances in contrastive learning, robust EEG decoding remains challenging because existing methods rely on fixed visual or textual anchors whose semantic relations may become misaligned with EEG representations that vary across trials, subjects, and learning stages. Our empirical evidence shows that this instability appears across both standard EEG decoding protocols and more challenging robustness settings, including strict cross-subject transfer and realistic personalized continual adaptation. We provide a formal analysis showing that fixed semantic supervision can bias optimization when EEG-specific relations evolve, and that structure-agnostic perturbations may distort semantically important EEG components. To address these issues, we propose Progressive Contrastive Alignment (ProCA), a unified and model-agnostic framework for adaptive neural-semantic alignment. ProCA progressively refines class-level contrastive supervision from frozen vision-language priors to EEG-aware semantic relations, and introduces structure-consistent interpolation to constrain feature mixing according to channel-wise and temporal importance. Across subject-dependent, subject-independent, strict cross-subject transfer, and continual adaptation settings, ProCA achieves average relative Top-1/Top-5 gains of 7.4%/3.9%, 10.0%/4.6%, 28.1%/17.8%, and 16.8%/11.6%, respectively.

</details>


### [304] [Compact Bellman-Grounded Cognitive Maps for Cost-Aware Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05104)
*Yuzhe Han,Mingkun Xu,Yujie Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BCM（Bellman-grounded Cognitive Map），一种可复用的认知地图模型。它通过自监督的Bellman目标将可加的异构路径代价写入紧凑的坐标编码中，无需针对每个目标重新训练即可支持不断变化的目标查询。在N=1600的加权网格上，BCM保持100%的成功率，平均Gap仅比精确Dijkstra搜索高5%，而基于连通性的谱基线约为45%。当图规模从N=400增至N=3600时，其内存占用呈亚线性增长且性能稳定，展现了良好的可扩展性，弥合了生物灵活性与最优路径规划之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物智能体面对熟悉环境时不需要为每个新目标重新求解路径，而是复用一次构建好的认知地图。现有的人工认知地图模型虽然也模仿了这种复用机制，但其导航引导并未显式地建立在可加的异构路径代价之上；同时，代表性的状态索引型和高秩谱构建方法在环境扩大时面临显著的内存增长问题。作者旨在解决这两个痛点：一是让地图引导真正基于异构路径代价，二是提高内存效率以实现可扩展性。

Method: BCM将可复用的认知地图建立在局部边代价之上，采用自监督的Bellman目标进行训练（将路径可达性/代价信号作为监督），并引入紧凑的坐标编码来降低表征维度，从而在支持可变目标查询的同时避免逐目标重训练。与依赖连通性的谱基线相比，BCM将代价信息显式写入地图表征。

Result: 在多达N=1600个节点的加权网格上，BCM保持完全（100%）的路径成功率和相对于精确Dijkstra仅约5%的平均Gap，而基于连通性的谱基线则约有45%的Gap。此外，当图规模从N=400增大到N=3600时，内存占用呈现亚线性增长，同时维持具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，可加的路径代价可以被写入紧凑、可复用的认知地图表征中，既保留了生物导航的灵活性与复用性，又达到了接近最优规划（Dijkstra）的性能，并具备亚线性内存的可扩展性，从而在生物学灵活性与最优路径规划之间建立了桥梁。

Abstract: Biological agents navigate familiar environments not by re-solving routes for each new goal, but by reusing a learned map built once and read off as goals change. Existing artificial cognitive-map models mimic this reuse, yet their guidance is not explicitly grounded in additive heterogeneous route costs. Furthermore, they often struggle with memory efficiency: representative state-indexed and high-rank spectral constructions incur substantial storage growth as the environment scales. We present BCM, which grounds a reusable cognitive map in local edge costs through a self-supervised Bellman-grounded objective and a compact coordinate encoding, supporting changing goal queries without per-goal retraining. On weighted grids of up to $N=1600$ nodes, BCM maintains full success and only a 5\% mean Gap relative to exact Dijkstra search, compared with about $45\%$ for a connectivity-based spectral baseline. Notably, as the graph size increases from $N=400$ to $N=3600$, its memory footprint grows sublinearly while maintaining competitive performance, making our method scalable to complex environments. Together, these results show that additive route costs can be written into a compact, reusable cognitive-map representation, bridging the gap between biological flexibility and optimal path planning.

</details>


### [305] [Unifying ICL, SFT, KL-Regularized RL Through a Bayesian Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05111)
*Junxin Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从贝叶斯视角统一了现代LLM的各种训练与评估范式（SFT、ICL、KL正则化RLHF/RLVR、OPD、测试时推理等）。核心是一个两步模板：构建基于先验/参考模型和效用信号的广义贝叶斯/吉布斯后验，然后通过前向KL投影将其近似到参数族上（权重内或上下文内）。文章揭示了ICL、SFT、RLHF/RLVR、奖励加权SFT、RW-ICL、AWSFT等方法的等价性（目标与一阶更新层面）与差异（学习信号来源与粒度），并阐述了其对现代推理管线（如冷启动必要性、DeepSeek-R1/o1式后验设计+投影）的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型现已在多种看似根本不同的范式下训练与评估（SFT、ICL、KL正则化RLHF/RLVR、OPD、测试时推理），而且近期实证结果（如few-shot提示对RL调优推理模型的混合影响）显得令人困惑。作者希望将这些方法置于同一理论框架下，以厘清它们之间真正的等价关系与本质差异，从而解释看似矛盾的经验现象。

Method: 提出一个统一的贝叶斯两步模板：(i) 利用先验/参考模型和效用信号（对数似然、奖励或优势）构建（广义）贝叶斯或吉布斯后验q*；(ii) 通过前向KL投影将q*近似到参数族上，投影可分在权重内（对应SFT/RL）或上下文内（对应ICL）。在Part I将few-shot ICL和SFT刻划为摊销的权重内投影到贝叶斯后验预测；Part II-IV证明KL正则化RLHF/RLVR、奖励加权SFT、RW-ICL和AWSFT都是对奖励或优势诱导后验的前向KL投影，并区分等价成立之处（目标、一阶更新）与不成立之处（信号来源、粒度）。Part V将上述框架应用于现代推理管线。

Result: 文章证明多种看似不同的训练范式实际上共享同一贝叶斯两步结构：都是对由奖励/优势诱导的后验做前向KL投影。等价性在一阶更新和目标层面成立，但在学习信号的来源与粒度层面不成立。基于此框架，作者解释了RLHF/RLVR配方可视为"后验设计+投影"，解释了冷启动或监督式预热对重要性加权KL投影的实践必要性，并将DeepSeek-R1和o1式推理模型刻画为测试时贝叶斯搜索与训练时KL摊销的结合。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯框架为现代LLM训练与推理范式的统一理解提供了理论基础，既能解释看似矛盾的实证现象，也给出了推理管线设计的指导原则（后验设计+投影、冷启动必要性等），同时也明确了各方法之间的边界（信号来源与粒度上的差异）。

Abstract: Large language models are now trained and evaluated under a diverse set of paradigms: supervised fine-tuning (SFT), few-shot in-context learning (ICL), KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR, on-policy distillation (OPD), and test-time reasoning with search and chain-of-thought. These methods are often discussed as fundamentally different, and recent empirical results--such as the mixed impact of few-shot prompting on RL-tuned reasoning models--can appear puzzling. This note develops a Bayesian perspective that puts these procedures on the same footing. At the core is a two-step template: (i) construct a (generalized) Bayes or Gibbs posterior q* over outputs or actions given a context, using a prior/reference model and a utility signal (log-likelihood, reward, or advantage); and (ii) approximate q* by a forward-KL projection onto a parametric family, either in-weights (SFT/RL) or in-context (ICL). Part I formalizes few-shot ICL and SFT as amortized and-weights projections onto the Bayes posterior predictive. Parts II-IV show that KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR, reward-weighted SFT, reward-weighted ICL (RW-ICL), and advantage-weighted SFT (AWSFT) are all instances of forward-KL projection onto posteriors induced by rewards or advantages. We disentangle where these equivalences hold (objectives and first-order updates) and where they do not (source and granularity of the learning signal). Part V sketches implications for modern reasoning pipelines: RLHF/RLVR recipes as "posterior design + projection", why cold-start or supervised warm-up is practically unavoidable for importance-weighted KL projections, and DeepSeek-R1 and o1-style reasoning models as combining test-time Bayesian search with training-time KL amortization.

</details>


### [306] [SciDocBench: A Workflow-Centered Benchmark and Data Pipeline for Scientific Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05141)
*Shenxi Wu,Yuhong Liu,Haosong Zhang,Tongjin Zou,Yanxun Zhang,Gaochang Chen,Dun Liang,Jiaqi Wang,Zhecan James Wang,Yuhang Zang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SciDocBench，一个以工作流为中心的科技文献理解基准，包含 124 道专家撰写且经过难度筛选的题目，涵盖 7 类研究助手能力组、19 个子任务、5 个科学领域；每个问题在四种匹配条件下实例化（中英文问题 × 纯图片优先/交错式文档表示），共形成 496 个评测实例。最强系统仅得 62.6/100 分，在文档感知、证据锚定、核验和跨文档推理上存在明显短板。基于此，作者提出 SciDocIR 有类型证据图表示与 SciDocDataset 训练数据（约 15K SFT 样本和 8K RL 样本），共同构成"评测到训练"的闭环框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学论文要求模型联合推理文本、公式、图表、代码和数据，并保留支撑证据的来源（provenance）。现有基准通常孤立地评测这些能力，无法判断多模态模型是否支持真实的科研阅读工作流，缺乏一个工作流中心、可控、可诊断的综合基准。

Method: 构建 SciDocBench 基准：124 道专家撰写并经难度筛选的问题，组织为 7 个研究助手能力组和 19 个子任务，覆盖 5 个科学领域；每个问题在四种匹配条件下实例化（英文/中文 × 全部图片优先/交错式文档表示），得到 496 个评测实例用于受控分析。进一步提出 SciDocIR，一种有类型证据图表示，保留科学文档对象、布局、交叉引用关系及来源；基于该表示构建 SciDocDataset，包含约 15K 有监督微调样本和 8K 强化学习样本，覆盖 14 个可验证子任务。

Result: 最强评测系统仅达到 62.6/100 分，在文档感知、证据锚定、核验和跨文档推理方面存在显著不足。SciDocDataset 提供了约 15K SFT 与 8K RL 训练样本，构成评测到训练的可扩展信号来源。

Conclusion: SciDocBench、SciDocIR 与 SciDocDataset 三者共同构成"评测到训练"（evaluation-to-training）的框架，可系统性地诊断并改进科学文档助手的能力，填补了现有基准在真实科研阅读工作流评测上的空白。

Abstract: Scientific papers require models to reason jointly over text, equations, figures, tables, code, and datasets while preserving the provenance of supporting evidence. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate these capabilities in isolation, leaving unclear whether multimodal models can support realistic scientific-reading workflows. We introduce SciDocBench, a workflow-centered benchmark for scientific document understanding. It contains 124 expert-authored and difficulty-screened questions organized into seven research-assistant capability groups and 19 subtasks across five scientific domains. Each question is instantiated under four matched conditions combining English or Chinese questions with all-images-first or interleaved document representations, yielding 496 evaluation instances for controlled analysis. The strongest evaluated system achieves only 62.6/100, with pronounced weaknesses in document perception, evidence grounding, verification, and cross-document reasoning. To translate these diagnostics into scalable training signals, we introduce SciDocIR, a typed evidence-graph representation that preserves scientific document objects, layout and cross-reference relations, and provenance. Building on SciDocIR, we construct SciDocDataset, comprising approximately 15K supervised fine-tuning samples and 8K reinforcement-learning samples across 14 verifiable subtasks. Together, SciDocBench, SciDocIR, and SciDocDataset form an evaluation-to-training framework for diagnosing and improving scientific-document assistants. The project page is available at https://github.com/InternLM/SciDocBench.

</details>


### [307] [A Hybrid Predictive Ensemble of Machine Learning and Deep Neural Networks for Early Cardiovascular Disease Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05146)
*Balaji Venkateswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合机器学习与深度神经网络集成的智能框架，利用IoMT设备采集的实时生理数据（ECG、心率、血压），通过预处理、特征选择和集成分类器（SVM、随机森林、XGBoost）实现心血管疾病的早期检测与预后，并在云基础设施上实现可扩展的实时监测，实验验证其准确性和临床决策支持能力优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病是全球主要致死原因之一，早期检测与预后对改善患者结局至关重要。传统诊断方法存在准确率不足、误报率高、一致性差等问题，且缺乏对实时、多源生理数据的有效利用，因此亟需一种能够整合传统机器学习与深度学习优势的智能诊断框架，以实现主动式医疗健康管理。

Method: 该框架首先通过IoMT设备（ECG传感器、心率监测器、血压追踪器）采集实时生理数据；随后执行预处理步骤，包括噪声去除、归一化和缺失值填补以保证数据质量；再通过有效的特征选择方法筛选最具显著性的健康指标；最后采用SVM、随机森林和XGBoost等优化分类器构建集成架构以提升诊断精度。整个系统部署于云基础设施上，支持实时处理与连续患者监控。

Result: 在真实心血管数据集上进行实验评估，框架在预测心血管疾病风险方面取得了更高的准确率、更低的假阳性率以及更强的结果一致性，相比传统方法表现优越，证实了其在早期风险评估和临床决策支持方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，将传统机器学习与深度学习范式相结合，能够有效实现前瞻性（主动式）医疗健康管理并改善患者预后，为心血管疾病的早期检测与持续监测提供了可靠且可扩展的智能解决方案。

Abstract: This study introduces an intelligent framework that integrates machine learning and deep neural network ensemble techniques for early detection and prognosis of cardiovascular diseases. The system utilizes real-time physiological data collected from Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) devices, including ECG sensors, heart rate monitors, and blood pressure trackers. To ensure the accuracy and reliability of input data, preprocessing steps such as noise reduction, normalization, and missing value imputation are employed. The most significant health indicators are identified through effective feature selection methods and then processed using optimized classifiers such as Support Vector Machines (SVM), Random Forests, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), which are combined in an ensemble architecture to improve diagnostic precision. The framework demonstrates remarkable performance in predicting cardiovascular disease risk, achieving higher accuracy, reduced false positives, and enhanced consistency compared to conventional methods. It is designed on a cloud-based infrastructure that ensures scalability and real-time processing for continuous patient monitoring. Experimental evaluation on real-world cardiovascular datasets confirms the framework's efficiency in early-stage risk assessment and clinical decision support. The results highlight the potential of combining traditional machine learning and deep learning paradigms to achieve proactive healthcare management and improve patient outcomes.

</details>


### [308] [Large Language Models for HVAC Operations in Building Energy Systems: A Critical Review of Methods, Applications, and Deployment Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05314)
*Alexander Neubauer,Tianzhen Hong,Han Li,Mengbo Yu,Amin Darbandi,Yannick Fürst,Martin Kriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本系统性综述编码分析了2023年至2026年3月间发表的66篇关于大型语言模型（LLM）用于HVAC（暖通空调）运维的同行评审研究。研究表明该领域语料集中于建筑能耗建模（BEM，占32篇），而负荷预测证据不足；仅有4项研究达到试点级证据水平，无任何研究达到持续部署；没有任何研究被认定为"可立即采用"，3项为近期可用、其余63项仅限研究阶段。LLM当前主要适合作为语义与工作流层（如点名称规范化、文档驱动的运维支持、BEM工作流辅助、基于物理控制器的咨询界面），而非自主HVAC控制器，未来需聚焦现场验证基准、运维约束下的编排评估以及带延迟上界和可验证安全属性的LLM-MPC/RL架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑自动化系统产生丰富的传感器数据，但因异构点命名、元数据缺失和文档碎片化等问题，其运行价值难以被真正挖掘，导致"数据丰富但洞察贫乏"。作者希望通过系统性综述，评估LLM技术在HVAC运维各子领域中的应用现状、证据等级与实际部署成熟度，以明确LLM在该领域应承担的责任边界（语义/工作流层 vs. 自主控制层）。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，对2023年至2026年3月间发表的66篇同行评审研究进行编码分析。每项研究从五个应用族和三个LLM方法族进行分类，并从三个维度评估：证据真实性（evidence realism）、部署就绪度（deployment readiness）、以及LLM与物理HVAC决策之间的责任边界。按证据等级和应用成熟度将研究划分为试点级、近期可用、研究专用等类别。

Result: 语料高度集中于BEM（32/66篇），负荷预测数据稀疏不足以得出子领域结论。仅4项研究达到试点级证据，无一项报告持续运行部署；无研究被认定为行业即用（ready-now），3项为近期可用，63项为研究专用。有若干有边界、人在环（human-in-the-loop）的应用值得近期试验（点名称规范化、文档驱动运维支持、BEM工作流辅助、围绕物理控制器的咨询界面）。相比之下，传统ML、MPC、RL和基于本体的工具在高频控制、短时域数值预测和良态本体映射方面仍更成熟；自主智能体运行与未验证的居住者代理仍处于研究阶段。整体证据支持LLM作为语义与工作流层而非自主HVAC控制器。

Conclusion: 当前证据表明，LLM在HVAC运维中的合理定位是语义理解与工作流辅助层，而非自主控制器。未来工作应优先推进现场验证的基准测试、在运行约束下评估编排（orchestration）性能，以及构建具有有界延迟和可验证安全属性的LLM-MPC/RL混合架构，从而缩小研究与实际部署之间的差距。

Abstract: Building automation systems generate rich sensor data yet remain insight-poor because heterogeneous point naming, missing metadata, and fragmented documentation obstruct their operational use. This systematic review analyses and codes 66 peer-reviewed studies on large language models (LLMs) for HVAC operations published between 2023 and March 2026. Each study is classified across five application families and three LLM method families and assessed for evidence realism, deployment readiness, and the responsibility boundary between the LLM and physical HVAC decisions. The corpus is concentrated in building energy modelling (BEM, 32 of 66 papers), while load forecasting remains too sparse for subfield-level conclusions. Only four studies reach pilot-level evidence, and none reports sustained operational deployment. No study was classified as ready-now for industry adoption; three were near-term and 63 research-only. Nevertheless, several bounded, human-in-the-loop uses merit near-term trials, including point-name normalisation, document-grounded operator support, BEM workflow assistance, and advisory interfaces around physics-based controllers. Conventional machine learning (ML), model predictive control (MPC), reinforcement learning (RL) and ontology-based tools remain more adopted for high-frequency control, short-horizon numerical forecasting, and well-posed ontology mapping, while autonomous agentic operation and unvalidated occupant proxies remain research-stage. Current evidence therefore supports LLMs primarily as semantic and workflow layers rather than autonomous HVAC controllers. Future work should prioritise field-validated benchmarks, orchestration evaluation under operational constraints, and LLM-MPC/RL architectures with bounded latency and verifiable safety properties.

</details>


### [309] [Interface-Induced Trajectory Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03966)
*Wenbo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 工具调用率并非模型本身的属性，而是“模型—接口—解析器—模板”整个服务栈共同作用的产物。仅仅更换服务端适配器（不改动权重、数据、解码和随机种子），同一模型在 BFCL v4 上可以从 0.00 跳到 0.96/0.19，且在 tau-bench 上从 0 个服务器解析调用变为 636 个。问题全部集中在聊天模板与解析器的交互项：单独修复任一侧都无效，必须同时配对修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测通常直接读取服务端日志中的工具调用率，但该指标可能被接口层“静默过滤”而完全失真：模型可能已经生成格式正确的调用，却在到达解析器/执行器之前被模板或解析逻辑截断。作者旨在证明这种静默失败普遍存在，并指出“工具调用率=0”可能不代表模型不会调用工具，而是测量栈本身存在缺陷。

Method: 作者采用控制变量实验：固定权重、测试样例、解码参数和随机种子，仅更换服务端适配器。在 BFCL v4 上做聊天模板×解析器的 2×2 消融，定位交互效应；在 tau-bench 的 115 个交互式任务上对比服务器解析调用数和实际执行任务数；在 Qwen2.5-Coder 的 21 倍规模范围（0.5B–32B）上复现漏斗，并用人工判定金标准校准；对 Llama-3.1-8B 测试严格模式标志的影响；在 verl 的 AgentLoop 训练循环中统计完整调用、被接受、执行和返回观测的数量。

Result: 1) BFCL v4 上主效应均为零，全部效应来自模板与解析器的交互，单独修任一侧均无收益；2) tau-bench 上更换适配器后，服务器解析调用从 0 增至 636，执行工具的任务从 0 增至 103；3) Qwen2.5-Coder 各规模下服务器解析均为 0/100，而模型实际生成的格式良好调用在 32B 时达 80/100（校准后约 72）；4) Llama-3.1-8B 的 23%任务自调用率在 strict:true 下降为 0；5) verl 7B AgentLoop 中 45/115 代包含完整调用，但 0 被接受、0 执行、0 返回观测；1.5B 的零是多重原因造成的。修复适配器后解析 0→84、救援 0→9、通过率 53→62（不显著）。

Conclusion: 工具调用率是模型与服务接口共同决定的联合属性，不能单独归因于模型能力。评测发布方必须报告聊天模板、解析器、适配器版本等完整栈配置，否则“0% 调用率”可能完全是测量伪影。作者提供的 98 行预检脚本可捕获此类静默失败，建议作为标准评测流程的前置检查。

Abstract: Agent evaluations report a tool-call rate read off the serving stack. That number can be zero while the model is emitting well-formed calls: the interface censors the trajectory before anything downstream sees it.
  On BFCL v4's own data, executor and scorer, holding weights, cases, decoding and seeds fixed and changing only the serving adapter, the same model scores 0.00 or 0.96 / 0.19. A 2x2 over chat template and parser locates the effect exactly: both main effects are exactly zero and all of it sits in the interaction -- no component is defective, and repairing one side of the contract buys precisely nothing. On tau-bench's 115 interactive retail tasks the same swap moves server-parsed calls from 0 to 636 and tasks reaching any tool execution from 0 to 103. Our probe reproduces the funnel across a 21x scale range of Qwen2.5-Coder: the server parses 0/100 at every size while well-formed emitted calls rise to 80/100 at 32B (~72 after calibration against an adjudicated gold standard). Under a matched envelope, across a comparable scale span, the silent fraction stays at 0-2, a prediction committed to the repository before the run. Llama-3.1-8B's 23% rate of calling the task function itself as a tool falls to 0 under one strict:true flag.
  The mismatch reaches inside the training loop, and its consequence is scale-dependent: in verl's AgentLoop at 7B, 45 of 115 generations carry a complete call; 0 are accepted, 0 execute, 0 return an observation. At 1.5B the same zero is over-determined, so we report the two scales separately. At evaluation time, repairing the adapter restores the mechanism but not a significant outcome gain: parsing 0->84, rescues 0->9, pass rate 53->62 (n.s.). We release a 98-line preflight check that catches every silent failure here. The observed tool-call rate is not a property of the model alone; it is a property of the model-interface stack that measures it.

</details>


### [310] [The Mirror Agent Model: a Bayesian Architecture for Interpretable Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05190)
*Michele Persiani,Thomas Hellström*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为“镜像智能体模型”（Mirror Agent Model）的新型架构，通过将观察者模型定义为智能体自身的镜像，生成可解释的行为和解释，并结合现成的显著性方法提供初步定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在让智能体的行为具有可解释性，并能够通过显式和隐式沟通传达意图，生成可读（legible）的行为，从而提升人机交互中的透明度与信任。

Method: 首先回顾先前关于意图信息沟通和可读行为生成的相关成果；然后为架构引入基于现成显著性方法的解释能力，并给出初步定性评估。

Result: 提供了初步的定性实验结果，展示该架构在生成解释和行为方面的能力。

Conclusion: 镜像智能体模型为可解释行为生成提供了一种新途径，未来可进一步扩展和验证。

Abstract: In this paper we illustrate a novel architecture generating interpretable behavior and explanations. We refer to this architecture as the Mirror Agent Model because it defines the observer model, that is the target of explicit and implicit communications, as a mirror of the agent's. With the goal of providing a general understanding of this work, we firstly show prior relevant results addressing the informative communication of agents intentions and the production of legible behavior. In the second part of the paper we furnish the architecture with novel capabilities for explanations through off-the-shelf saliency methods, followed by preliminary qualitative results.

</details>


### [311] [Technical Manual for a Toolkit for Measuring Contextual Individuation in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05333)
*José Luciano Verçosa Marques,Frederico Jorge Heitmann,Daniel Omar Perez,Marcelo Vinicius de Paula,Tárcio André dos Santos Barros*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文档介绍了一个开放工具包，其核心是“桥接词形”（bridge form）：一个拼写不变但跨领域词义不同的词。工具包完整流水线包括词形与领域声明、Wikipedia语料获取、词例定位、逐层表示提取、领域配对轮廓测量和配对可视化协议。每个设计选择都对应避免的方法学失败模式（如类别标签过宽导致义项污染、轮廓系数多组偏差、子词切分错位、降维轴可比性伪影）。本文仅作方法与实现参考，不报告实证结果，工具及语料归档供后续研究引用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型在嵌入层为每个词类型分配一个固定向量，但普遍认为后续层会按上下文区分该词的各次出现。为严格检验这一信念，需要一个“词形固定、语境与义项受控变化”的构造。桥接词形正是为此设计，从而支撑可复现、可标注的表示分离研究。

Method: 构建开放工具包，流水线含：声明式定义桥接词形及其源领域；从Wikipedia获取语料；定位词例；提取逐层表示；用领域配对轮廓法度量表示空间分离度；采用配对可视化协议。每一步均明确说明其规避的失败模式（广义类别标签的义项污染、轮廓系数多组偏差、子词分词错位、降维图轴可比性伪影等）。

Result: 本文不报告或解释任何模型或桥接词形集上的实证结果；侧重交付方法学与实现参考。工具包、完整源码及用于运行的语料已以持久标识符归档，可作为研究仪器被后续实证研究引用。

Conclusion: 该文档是一份方法参考与实现手册，为后续使用该工具包开展实证研究提供了规范化的构造和测量流程，并将工具本身作为可引用资源发布。

Abstract: A transformer language model assigns a single, context-independent vector to a word type at its embedding layer, yet is widely believed to individuate that word's occurrences by context in its later layers. Testing this belief cleanly requires a construct that holds the word form fixed while its context and intended sense vary in a controlled, labeled way. This manual documents an open toolkit built around such a construct, which we call a bridge form: a single written word that recurs, unchanged, across two or more subject domains with a different sense in each. We describe, and justify, every stage of the pipeline: the declarative specification of bridge forms and their source domains, corpus acquisition from Wikipedia, occurrence localization, layer-wise representation extraction, a domain-pairwise silhouette measurement of separation in the model's representation space, and a paired visualization protocol. Each design choice is presented together with the methodological failure mode it is meant to avoid (sense contamination from overly broad category labels, the multi-group bias of the silhouette coefficient, subword-tokenization misalignment, and axis-comparability artifacts in dimensionality-reduced plots, among others). This manuscript is a methodological and implementation reference: it does not report or interpret empirical outcomes of running the toolkit on any particular model or bridge-form set. The toolkit, its full source, and the corpora used to exercise it are archived separately (Section 9) under a persistent identifier, and are intended to be cited as an instrument by studies that use it to produce and interpret empirical results.

</details>


### [312] [What Matters in On-Policy Distillation? A Perspective on Data Efficiency and Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05198)
*Zhinan Hou,Jiaqi Zhang,Xunliang Cai,Keyou You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对On-Policy Distillation (OPD)中的数据效率与数据选择机制进行了实证研究，发现1-shot OPD（仅用单个示例训练）在所有采样示例下均稳定有效，且较难的示例往往带来更显著的性能提升。进一步分析表明，学生模型改进的真正驱动力并非高token熵，而是困难问题天然生成的长CoT路径。基于此，作者提出一种仅选择困难示例的简单数据选择方法，仅用8个精选困难示例即可匹配17K数据集基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD已成为推理领域增强大语言模型的广泛采用的后训练范式，但其数据中心的机制（data-centric mechanisms）仍相对未被充分探索。作者旨在通过实证研究填补这一空白，理解数据效率与数据选择在OPD中的作用。

Method: 首先在极端设置（1-shot OPD，仅用1个示例训练）下进行实验，发现其对所有采样示例均有效且更难的示例效果更好。接着分析改进的实际驱动因素，通过对比高token熵与长CoT路径的作用，发现长CoT才是关键——它能帮助学生模型在长推理视野上保持与教师的紧密对齐，并学习短CoT中缺失的批判性思维模式（如反思、'Alternatively'等）。据此提出仅选择困难示例（包括完全超出教师能力的'unsolvable'示例）的简单数据选择方法。

Result: 在四个规模从1.5B到7B的模型上进行的实验表明，仅用8个精选困难示例训练学生模型，即可匹配17K数据集基线的性能。

Conclusion: OPD的数据效率远高于预期，困难示例（通过其长CoT路径）是改进的核心驱动。数据选择策略应优先挑选困难乃至'unsolvable'的示例，这能大幅减少训练数据需求而保持性能，为OPD的数据中心优化提供了新的路径。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has emerged as a widely adopted post-training paradigm for enhancing large language models in reasoning domains. However, the data-centric mechanisms in OPD remain relatively underexplored. This paper presents a empirical study of data efficiency and data selection in OPD. We begin by investigating an extreme setting: training OPD on only one example, namely 1-shot OPD. Surprisingly, we find that 1-shot OPD is consistently effective across all sampled training examples and harder examples often yield superior performance gain. We next investigate what actually drives the student model's improvement in the training data. Our analysis reveals that the improvement is not driven by high token entropy, but the longer CoT paths which hard problems naturally generate. Training on longer CoT can help maintain closer alignment with the teacher over a long reasoning horizon, and learn critical thinking patterns usually missing in short CoTs, such as reflection (e.g., ``Alternatively''). Based on these insights, we propose a simple data selection method that selects only hard examples for training, where even ``unsolvable'' examples that completely exceed the teacher's capability can be successfully used. Our experiments conducted on four models ranging from 1.5B to 7B show that training the student model on only 8 selected hard examples matches the performance of the 17K dataset baseline.

</details>


### [313] [Does Your Agent's Memory Survive a Model Upgrade? A Controlled Study of Memory Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05339)
*Ankit Goyal,Jaideep Ray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究模型升级时不同记忆存储格式（长上下文原文LC-RAW、分块检索增强RAG、压缩笔记NOTES、固定模式知识图谱KG-fixed）在写作模型更换、嵌入迁移和修复场景下的迁移可靠性与信息丢失特性。实验基于48条合成历史、精确评分和两个小于10B参数的开源模型，发现固定模式知识图谱迁移最稳定（准确率变化仅+0.0004±0.0020），压缩笔记对模型耦合极高（迁移方向不对称地偏移+9.91或-13.28个百分点），部分嵌入迁移（50/50混合索引）仅捕获4.96点增益而错失全部重嵌入的11.90点，且仅存笔记的修复在所有48例中均无法达到90%恢复目标，而保留原始历史可在部分方向成功修复34/48例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型升级是常规操作，但记忆迁移却常被忽视——同一记忆库在新模型下可能被重新解读、混合嵌入版本可能破坏检索、失去原始证据后难以修复。作者旨在系统比较不同记忆表示（原文、RAG分块、压缩笔记、固定模式知识图谱）在模型更替与迁移中的表现，揭示其可靠性差异与失败根因。

Method: 采用受控实验对比四种记忆形式：LC-RAW（原文直接长上下文读取）、RAG（分块+检索增强生成）、NOTES（模型压缩为自然语言笔记）、KG-fixed（归一化为固定模式知识图谱）。使用48条合成历史（含随机化答案编码）、精确评分、两个<10B参数的开源模型，并设计了写作模型互换（writer swap）、50/50混合索引的部分嵌入迁移、以及仅保留记忆库（store-only）与保留原始历史两种修复条件。通过诊断分解量化信息丢失（构建期损失）与检索失败对各自准确率缺口的贡献。

Result: KG-fixed迁移最可靠（准确率仅变化+0.0004±0.0020）；NOTES对模型高度耦合，迁移方向导致+9.91或-13.28个百分点的非对称偏移；RAG部分迁移（50/50混合索引）仅提升4.96点，缺失了全重嵌入11.90点增益的多数；诊断将NOTES缺口80%（0.467±0.014）归因于构建期信息丢失，而RAG缺口81%（0.364±0.012）源于检索失败；仅存笔记的修复在全部48例中未达90%恢复目标，保留原始历史则在某一方向成功恢复34/48例。

Conclusion: 研究强调：固定模式结构可跨模型可靠迁移，而压缩式笔记与模型强耦合且不可靠；嵌入迁移必须保持严格的向量空间隔离（部分混合迁移收益有限）；记忆修复必须保留原始源历史作为证据基础。这提示实践上需进行方向特定的迁移测试、严格嵌入空间隔离以及源历史留存策略。

Abstract: Model upgrades are routine; memory migrations are not. An agent can keep the same memory store and still forget: a new model may interpret old notes differently, mixed embedding versions may break retrieval, and repair may fail without the original evidence. We compare memory as the same history is preserved verbatim for long-context reading (LC-RAW), divided into chunks for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), compressed by a model into natural-language notes (NOTES), or normalized into a fixed-schema knowledge graph (KG-fixed). The study uses 48 synthetic histories with randomized answer codes, exact scoring, and two open-weight models with sub 10 billion parameters.
  Our measurements show that fixed-schema structures transfer reliably, with KG-fixed accuracy changing by only $+0.0004 \pm 0.0020$ following a writer swap. Conversely, compressed NOTES exhibit high model coupling, with accuracy shifting asymmetrically by $+9.91$ or $-13.28$ percentage points depending on the specific migration direction. In RAG systems, partial embedding migrations using a 50/50 mixed index capture only a 4.96-point accuracy improvement, forfeiting the majority of the 11.90-point gain achieved through full re-embedding. Diagnostic decomposition attributes 80% ($0.467 \pm 0.014$) of the NOTES accuracy deficit to information lost during initial construction, whereas retrieval failures drive 81% ($0.364 \pm 0.012$) of the RAG deficit. Finally, store-only repair of NOTES fails to reach a 90% performance recovery target in all 48 test cases, whereas retaining the raw source history enables successful recovery in 34 of 48 cases for one tested direction. These findings highlight the necessity of direction-specific migration testing, strict embedding space isolation, and the retention of source histories for memory repair.

</details>


### [314] [CABAL: Multi-Agent Simulacra for Tracing the Effects of Collusive Bidding in Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05227)
*Jicheng Zhou,Kemou Li,Kahim Wong,Zheyuan Li,Zhuan Shi,Fengpeng Li,Haiwei Wu,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个端到端多智能体模拟框架，用于研究会议审稿分配中合谋投标的完整生命周期影响，并开发一种基于亲和力的合谋投标策略，实验表明该策略能显著提高目标论文被分配的几率，但现有检测器难以有效识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将投标、分配和评审操纵视为独立阶段，忽视合谋投标的端到端效应；同时真实数据中合谋意图不可观测且缺少同一会议的反事实，难以进行实证分析。

Method: 构建端到端多智能体模拟框架，固定会议环境，配置基于LLM的评审代理（诚实或合谋策略）；提出亲和力引导的合谋投标策略，利用评审-论文相互亲和度构建合谋环并选择目标论文，生成与专业水平一致的攻击而非任意投标。

Result: 合谋投标使目标论文被分配的几率提高一倍以上；被分配的合谋者给目标论文的评分平均比诚实共审者高约两分；会议整体影响相对较小；现有投标阶段检测器证据有限，固定三元组检测压力测试中正投标图被良性亲和混淆，而Very-High专用视图能精确但低覆盖率地局部恢复。

Conclusion: 合谋投标在完整生命周期中具有实质性但局部化的影响，现有检测手段难以兼顾准确率与覆盖率，需要更新更强的防御机制来保障审稿分配完整性。

Abstract: Recent reports during the AAAI-27 review cycle highlight the risk of reviewers coordinating bids for reciprocal assignment advantage. Prior work treats bidding, reviewer assignment, and review manipulation as separate stages, leaving the lifecycle effects of collusive bidding unclear. Real-world analysis is further constrained by typically unobservable collusive intent and the lack of counterfactuals for the same conference. Motivated by this gap, we introduce \alg, an end-to-end multi-agent simulacra framework for studying reviewer assignment integrity by holding the conference environment fixed and configuring LLM-driven reviewer agents with honest or collusive policies. We further develop an affinity-guided collusive bidding strategy that uses mutual reviewer-paper affinities to construct collusion rings and select target papers, producing expertise-consistent rather than arbitrarily targeted attacks. Controlled experiments show that collusive bidding more than doubles target-paper capture and that assigned colluders score target papers about two points higher than honest co-reviewers, while conference-wide effects remain comparatively modest. Evaluated bid-phase detectors provide only limited evidence of collusion: in a fixed-triplet detector stress test, native positive-bid graphs are confounded by benign affinity, while a Very-High-only diagnostic view enables precise but low-coverage local recovery.

</details>


### [315] [Multi-Step Tool-Calling over Korean Open Public APIs: A Benchmark and a Data-Synthesis Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05395)
*Dain Kim,Eungi Cho,Kyumin Kim,Shinyeong Noh,Kyuseong Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KOPA-Bench基准（145个真实任务）衡量开源模型在政府API多步工具调用上的表现，并引入EDGE方法：基于实时执行验证构建动态图，仅保留真实可用的工具间数据流链接，用于合成可执行的多步轨迹，通过GRPO微调9B模型，使其接近27B模型性能，且在KOPA-Bench和BFCL上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据主权法规要求公共机构部署开源本地LLM代理，需链式调用多个政府API。但开源模型在多步场景下性能落后，且没有现有基准量化这一差距，因此需要新基准和相应改进方法。

Method: 构建KOPA-Bench（145个真实任务）；提出EDGE（Execution-grounded Dynamic Graph）：通过实际调用实时API，构建工具输出到输入的有向图，仅保留真实成功链接，遍历这些已验证链接合成可执行的多步轨迹；用GRPO对合成数据微调9B模型。

Result: EDGE微调的9B模型在多步工具调用上接近未调优的27B同族模型，并在KOPA-Bench和BFCL基准上均有大幅改进。

Conclusion: 基于实时执行验证的数据合成与RL微调可有效缩小开源小模型与更大模型在多步政府API调用上的差距，为受数据主权约束的部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: Data-sovereignty regulations increasingly require public institutions to deploy open-source, on-premise LLM agents that chain multiple tool-calls across live government APIs. However, open-source models consistently underperform in this multi-step setting, and no existing benchmark measures the gap. We introduce the Korean Open Public API Benchmark (KOPA-Bench), comprising 145 real-world tasks. To close this gap, we present EDGE, an Execution-grounded Dynamic Graph for tool-calling data synthEsis driven by live execution. EDGE builds a graph of how each tool's output can feed another's input, keeps only the links that succeed when actually called against the live APIs, and traverses these verified links to synthesize executable multi-step trajectories. Fine-tuned via GRPO on the resulting dataset, our 9B model nearly matches the untuned 27B model from the same family, improving substantially not only on KOPA-Bench but also on the BFCL benchmark.

</details>


### [316] [ACE: Adaptive Calibration-Free Expert Skipping for MoE-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05228)
*Zukang Xu,Zhixiong Zhao,Xing Hu,Jiangyong Yu,Houji Wen,Jun Li,Zhe Jiang,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ACE框架，实现MoE大语言模型中免训练、免校准、保持检查点的token自适应专家跳过方法。通过全局谱代理(GSP)与路由器条件细化(RCR)两个互补组件，结合离线计算的专家统计量（仅需在线查表与轻量标量运算），在推理时仅当两个视图同时判定某专家槽低贡献时才跳过，且始终保留top-1专家。在三个MoE模型与八个基准上，ACE优于现有静态与动态基线，在高比例跳过时优势更明显（如Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上50%跳过率时困惑度降低7.96%，下游准确率提升4.15个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构为扩展LLM提供高效范式，但固定的top-k路由对每个token激活相同数量的专家槽，导致大量冗余计算。现有专家跳过方法依赖路由器置信度、校准数据或额外训练，无法可靠估计被路由专家的实际贡献，因此需要一种无需训练、校准且保留检查点的可靠专家跳过方法。

Method: ACE框架包含两个互补组件：1) 全局谱代理(GSP)，从耦合的gate、up、down投影结合RMSNorm缩放估计全局变换能力；2) 路由器条件细化(RCR)，从居中路由器权重构建专家特定方向原型，并沿路由偏好方向评估专家响应。推理时结合两者估计与运行时路由器gate，仅当两个视图都识别某专家槽为低贡献时跳过，始终保留top-1专家。所有专家统计量离线计算，在线仅需查表和轻量标量运算，免训练、免校准、保留检查点。

Result: 在三个MoE-based LLM和八个基准上的大量实验表明，ACE一致优于现有静态与动态基线，在激进专家跳过下优势愈发明显。例如在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上50%跳过率时，ACE将WikiText-2困惑度降低7.96%，下游平均准确率较最强竞争方法提升4.15个百分点。

Conclusion: ACE通过免训练、免校准、保留检查点的双重视图（谱能力估计与路由条件细化）机制，提供了可靠的token自适应专家跳过方案，在保证模型输出质量的同时显著降低冗余计算，并随跳过比例增大展现出越加明显的性能优势。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures provide an efficient paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs), yet fixed top-k routing activates the same number of expert slots for every token, causing substantial redundant computation. Existing expert-skipping methods often rely on router confidence, calibration data, or additional training, and therefore cannot reliably estimate the actual contribution of routed experts. To this end, we propose ACE, a training-free, calibration-free, and checkpoint-preserving framework for token-adaptive expert skipping in MoE-based LLMs. ACE contains two complementary components: 1) Global Spectral Proxy (GSP), which estimates global transformation capacity from the coupled gate, up, and down projections together with RMSNorm scaling; and 2) Router-Conditioned Refinement (RCR), which constructs expert-specific direction prototypes from centered router weights and evaluates expert responses along routing-preferred directions. During inference, ACE combines both estimates with runtime router gates and skips an expert slot only when both views identify it as low-contribution, while always retaining the top-1 expert. All expert statistics are computed offline, leaving only table lookups and lightweight scalar operations online. Extensive experiments across three MoE-based LLMs and eight benchmarks demonstrate that ACE consistently outperforms existing static and dynamic baselines, with increasingly pronounced advantages under aggressive expert skipping. For instance, at a 50% skipping ratio on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, ACE reduces WikiText-2 perplexity by 7.96% and improves average downstream accuracy by 4.15 percentage points over the strongest competing method.

</details>


### [317] [Substrate-Aware AI Agents: Execution Context as a First-Class Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05232)
*Manu Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“基底盲区”（substrate blindness）概念：自主AI智能体在规划时忽略执行环境的内存、运行时、计算和操作约束。通过在数值代码生成任务中测试，向Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6-Sol和Gemini 3.7 Flash提供显式的内存与时间契约后，生成的代码在峰值内存和墙钟时间上显著改善，符合预算的成功率大幅提高，验证了执行契约驱动的主动结构适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体在选择行动时，通常不考虑执行环境的具体约束（内存、运行时间、计算和操作条件），这种缺失会导致生成的计划或代码在实际运行中资源消耗过高或不符合要求。

Method: 以数值代码生成（高维成对欧氏距离任务）为受控试验，比较三种前沿模型（Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6-Sol、Gemini 3.7 Flash）在仅给定任务与同时给定任务加128MB RAM/10.0秒墙钟契约两种情况下的生成代码。审计结构变化，并用更紧的96MB契约重复独立采样，测量峰值内存（MaxRSS）和墙钟时间。

Result: 契约披露使14个可执行索引对齐比较中13个降低峰值进程内存，所有三个队列的墙钟时间均值降低，执行最多快3.1倍。结构变化包括有界分块、float32保留、上三角遍历、原地或内存映射缓冲。96MB契约下，契约披露组达标率（4/5、5/5、3/5）显著高于仅任务组（0/5、1/5、0/5）；队列平均MaxRSS和墙钟时间分别比仅任务参照低49-74%和35-64%。

Conclusion: 最小执行契约能诱导生成程序主动进行结构适应，使计算远离无约束分配，在运行前明显改善资源-时间轮廓，为基底感知的智能体规划提供了受控概念验证。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly select actions in environments whose memory, execution-time, runtime, compute, and operational constraints determine what counts as a suitable plan. We call the absence of this execution context from an agent's planning state substrate blindness. We test this general proposition through numerical code generation, where selected implementation choices and operational consequences are directly observable. Three frontier model configurations--Anthropic Claude Opus 5, OpenAI GPT-5.6-Sol, and Google Gemini 3.7 Flash--generate code for a high-dimensional pairwise Euclidean-distance task either from the task alone or with a 128 MB RAM and 10.0 s wall-time contract. Contract disclosure reduced measured peak process memory in 13 of 14 executable index-aligned task-only versus contract-disclosed comparisons and reduced mean wall time in all three cohorts, making execution up to 3.1x faster. Across the audited corpus, disclosure produced structural code changes including bounded blocking, float32 retention, upper-triangle traversal, and in-place or memory-mapped buffers. At a tighter 96 MB contract, independently sampled contract-disclosed cohorts achieved correct-and-within-budget outcomes of 4/5 for Claude Opus 5, 5/5 for GPT-5.6-Sol, and 3/5 for Gemini 3.7 Flash, compared with task-only outcomes of 0/5, 1/5, and 0/5; cohort mean MaxRSS and wall time were 49-74% and 35-64% lower than their task-only references. These results establish a controlled proof of concept for substrate-aware agent planning: a minimal execution contract induces proactive structural adaptation in generated programs, shifting computation away from unconstrained allocations and substantially improving observed resource-time profiles before execution.

</details>


### [318] [Uncensored Open-weight Models: Redistribution as the Persistence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05241)
*10a Labs,:,Juliette Garcia,Hailey May,Bobby McKenzie,David Pham,Matthew Swain,Joshua Valdez,Corie Wieland,Zachary Yahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究描绘了一个迅速扩张的"去护栏"AI模型生态系统：在2024年1月至2026年3月期间，研究者从HuggingFace上识别出3,471个原始"无审查"开源模型，这些模型平均被重新打包2.4次，共产生8,164次压缩再分发；其中三家参与者贡献了52%的再分发量。经过量化压缩并跨账户、格式和Ollama等注册表镜像传播后，这些模型即使上游被移除仍能持久存在，且更易于下游部署。在所识别的1,643个集成"无审查大语言模型"（ULLM）的GitHub应用中，25%被判定为明确恶意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着开源权重AI模型的普及，越来越多行为者正在移除其内置的安全护栏（guardrails），形成所谓"无审查模型"的扩张生态。该研究的动机在于系统性地绘制这一生态的图谱，理解这些去护栏模型的产生、复制、传播与应用链条，以揭示其规模与潜在威胁——这是AI安全与治理领域此前缺乏系统性量化认知的重要空白。

Method: 研究者采用系统性数据挖掘与生态画像方法：(1) 在HuggingFace平台上对2024年1月至2026年3月期间进行大规模识别，筛出3,471个原始"无审查"模型；(2) 追踪这些模型的下游再打包（repackaging）与压缩再分发（quantization及镜像传播）行为，统计跨账户、跨格式、跨注册表（如Ollama）的复制链条；(3) 对GitHub上的集成应用进行大规模抓取与分类，识别1,643个集成ULLM的应用，并对其中是否显式恶意进行判定分类。

Result: 主要发现包括：共识别3,471个原始无审查模型，平均每个被重新打包2.4次，累计产生8,164次压缩再分发；生态高度集中，仅三家参与者就占据52%的再分发量。量化压缩与跨注册表镜像使这些模型在被上游删除后仍能持久存在并降低下游部署门槛。GitHub上1,643个集成ULLM的应用中，25%被明确归类为恶意应用，表明该生态不仅规模可观，且具有实质性的安全威胁。

Conclusion: 研究结论揭示了一个结构性安全隐患：去护栏开源AI模型的生态具有高度集中、自我复制和持久化传播的特征，使其难以通过删除源头来根除；同时相当比例的下游应用是明确恶意的，构成了现实的AI滥用风险。这提示AI安全治理需要超越"移除源头模型"的简单做法，而应对再分发链条、压缩镜像和恶意下游应用采取系统性、生态级的应对策略。

Abstract: A rapidly expanding ecosystem of actors is removing built-in safety guardrails from open-weight AI models. We profile this ecosystem by identifying key producers, downstream reproductions, and emerging applications. Between January 2024 and March 2026, we identified 3,471 original uncensored models on HuggingFace, each repackaged an average of 2.4 times; three actors account for 52% of all 8,164 compressed redistributions. Once quantized and mirrored across separate accounts, formats, and registries such as Ollama, these models persist regardless of upstream removal and become easier to deploy downstream. Of the 1,643 identified GitHub applications integrating uncensored large language models (ULLMs), 25% were classified as explicitly malicious.

</details>


### [319] [Do LLMs Exhibit Coherent Knowledge Structures in Mathematical Reasoning? A Perspective from Knowledge Space Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05245)
*Peng Cui,Heejin Do,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于知识空间理论（KST）构建了一个评估框架，用以检验大语言模型（LLM）在数学推理中的知识结构是否符合人类知识依赖关系。对八个开源和闭源LLM与真实人类学习者的对比评估表明，LLM既不遵循人类知识结构（频繁违反知识依赖、无法利用上下文中的相关知识提升表现），彼此之间也不具有一致的知识结构；且这些结构性缺陷在基于准确率和LLM-as-judge的评估中几乎不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类知识本质上是结构化且相互依赖的：掌握一个概念需要先掌握其前置概念，这由知识空间理论（KST）形式化。尽管LLM在复杂推理任务上表现强劲，但其是否具备连贯、类人的知识结构尚不清楚。作者因此希望从知识结构的角度，而非单纯的任务准确率，来审视LLM的推理能力。

Method: 作者引入一个基于KST的框架来评估LLM在数学推理中的知识结构，将其作为规范性框架分析LLM行为是否遵循原则性的知识依赖关系。实验选取八个开源与闭源LLM，与真实人类学习者进行对照，考察其知识分布、对知识依赖的遵从程度、以及利用上下文相关信息的能力，并与基于准确率和LLM-as-judge的评估结果进行对比。

Result: 研究发现：（1）LLM不遵循人类知识结构——频繁违反知识依赖关系，且无法利用上下文提供的相关知识来提升依赖问题的表现；（2）不同LLM之间缺乏一致的知识结构，知识分布重叠度很低；（3）这些结构性缺陷在基于准确率和LLM-as-judge的评估中几乎无法被察觉。

Conclusion: 以上结果提供了行为层面的证据，表明当前LLM的知识并不遵循类人的结构。这一发现凸显了传统准确率指标在评估LLM能力上的局限性，并说明需要引入基于知识结构的评估维度来更全面地刻画LLM的推理能力。

Abstract: Human knowledge is inherently structured and interdependent: mastery of a concept requires prior mastery of its prerequisites, a principle formalized by Knowledge Space Theory (KST). While LLMs achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks, it remains unclear whether they exhibit coherent, human-like knowledge structure. We introduce a KST-grounded framework for evaluating LLM knowledge structure in mathematical reasoning, using it as a normative framework to analyze whether LLM behavior adheres to principled knowledge dependencies. Evaluating eight open- and closed-source LLMs against real human learners, we find that (1) LLMs do not adhere to human knowledge structure -- they frequently violate knowledge dependencies and fail to leverage related knowledge provided in context to improve performance on dependent questions; (2) LLMs do not share a consistent knowledge structure among themselves, as reflected by low overlap in their knowledge distributions. Furthermore, these structural deficiencies remain largely invisible to accuracy-based and LLM-as-judge evaluations. Together, our results provide behavioral evidence that current LLMs knowledge does not follow a human-like structure.

</details>


### [320] [A Unified Physics-Aware Quantum Machine Learning Framework across Power GaN HEMTs and Logic Nanowire FETs: Predicting Unseen Process Splits and Held-Out Geometry Combinations with Lower Error and Tighter Split-to-Split Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05251)
*Rushat Rai,Yun-Yuan Wang,Autsada Kakaen,Pei-Jie Chang,Doan Viet Nguyen,Yuan-Chieh Chiu,Doldet Tantraviwat,Niall Tumilty,Simon See,Wen-Jay Lee,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,Tian-Li Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出统一强化学习框架，通过图神经网络策略和PPO优化，搜索紧凑参数化量子电路（PQC）用于数据稀缺器件建模，以LOGOCV误差为奖励，在11个目标上优于六种经典基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据稀缺的半导体器件建模场景中，传统方法（如经典代理模型）存在误差大、跨组泛化（OOD）能力弱和物理一致性不足的问题，需要更紧凑且泛化能力强的替代模型。

Method: 使用深度强化学习：策略为图神经网络（GNN），优化算法为PPO；通过留一组交叉验证（LOGOCV）在未见过的工艺/几何组上的误差作为奖励信号，自动搜索PQC电路架构。

Result: 在全部11个目标上MAE最低；对HEMT器件，Ioff误差降低59%，VTH折叠变异性收紧81%；对NWFET器件，VTH、SS、Ioff误差降低84%，Ioff折叠变异性收紧82%。

Conclusion: RL选择的经典模拟PQC可作为紧凑代理模型，在不施加显式物理约束或器件方程的情况下，实现低OOD误差和更好的物理一致性，展示了在器件建模中的潜力。

Abstract: We present a unified reinforcement-learning (RL) framework that discovers compact parametrized quantum circuits (PQCs) for data-scarce device modeling. A graph neural network (GNN) policy optimized by proximal policy optimization (PPO) searches circuit architectures using leave-one-group-out cross-validation (LOGOCV) error on held-out process or geometry groups as the reward. The framework achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) on all 11 targets versus six classical baselines, with 59% lower error (Ioff) and 81% tighter fold variability (VTH) for HEMTs and 84% lower error (VTH, SS, Ioff) and 82% tighter fold variability (Ioff) for NWFETs. These results demonstrate the potential of RL-selected, classically simulated PQCs as compact surrogates with low OOD error and improved physical consistency, despite imposing no explicit physical constraints, penalty terms, or device-specific equations, on the two evaluated device datasets.

</details>


### [321] [Commonsense Reasoning in Computer Vision: Foundations, Recent Advancements, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05257)
*Bahar Uddin Mahmud,Sumit Barua,Guan Yue Hong,Ajay Gupta,Hexu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于将常识知识整合到计算机视觉任务中的综合性综述，系统回顾了基于知识图谱、场景图谱、神经符号模型和常识增强Transformer的方法，并指出了数据集偏差、知识不完整性和集成挑战等局限，最后展望了跨模态推理、可扩展常识知识注入和神经符号混合架构等未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于CNN的视觉模型只能识别图像中的具体物体，缺乏对场景整体性的理解。将常识知识融入视觉理解，可让模型以更整体的方式解释场景，提升物体间关系和动作的空间推理能力，从而改善物体识别、上下文理解及现实应用中的精度和交互。本文旨在系统梳理这一领域的发展现状。

Method: 本文采用系统性综述方法，对近年来将常识知识整合进计算机视觉任务的研究进行分类梳理，主要涵盖四类方法：基于知识图谱的方法、基于场景图谱的方法、神经符号模型以及常识增强Transformer。同时从数据集偏差、知识不完整性和集成挑战三个维度归纳当前局限。

Result: 综述系统呈现了常识知识赋能计算机视觉的多种技术路线，揭示了各类方法的优势与不足，明确了当前面临的三大核心挑战（数据集偏差、知识不完整性、集成困难），并据此提出跨模态推理、可扩展常识知识注入和神经符号混合架构三个前瞻研究方向。

Conclusion: 将常识知识融入计算机视觉是构建真正智能视觉系统的关键路径。尽管已有诸多进展，仍需解决数据与知识的瓶颈以及融合难题；未来的跨模态推理、可扩展知识注入和神经符号混合架构有望推动更完备、更智能的视觉理解系统的发展。

Abstract: Commonsense reasoning in computer vision encompasses integrating visual data and contextual knowledge, crucial for enhancing AI's understanding of everyday scenarios. This understanding not only improves machine learning models but also enhances their ability to interact meaningfully with humans and the environment. Unlike CNN-based conventional vision models, which are designed to identify objects within a specific image, incorporating commonsense knowledge enables models to interpret scenes in a more holistic manner, thereby improving their spatial ability to reason about relationships among objects and actions. This integration not only enhances object recognition but also facilitates a deeper understanding of the contextual factors, ultimately leading to more precise predictions and interactions in real-world applications. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of recent developments that integrate commonsense knowledge into computer vision tasks. We systematically review approaches based on knowledge graphs, scene graphs, neuro-symbolic models, and commonsense-augmented transformers. We also outline current limitations related to dataset bias, knowledge incompleteness, and integration challenges. Finally, we highlight prospective research trajectories in cross-modal reasoning, scalable commonsense knowledge injection, and neuro-symbolic hybrid architectures to develop truly intelligent visual systems.

</details>


### [322] [Trace2Tower: Transition-Aware EigenTrace Induction of Multi-Level Skills for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05261)
*Jiazheng Sun,Boyu Yang,Binhao Yuan,Mingxuan Li,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trace2Tower 是一个基于转换感知的 EigenTrace（特征轨迹谱分解）框架，通过将原始执行轨迹蒸馏成规范事件并构建语义兼容、转换动力学与结果证据统一驱动的图，再借助对比谱分解分离出稳定且对齐成功的行动模式、抑制易失败的捷径，从而自组织成由动作模板、程序化例程与高层任务策略构成的动态技能塔，并经由验证器引导的反馈持续精炼。在 ALFWorld 上实现 87.31% 的成功率、仅需 10.35 步和 0.26 次无效动作，在 WebShop 上实现 50.67% 的精确成功率，全面超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体依赖执行轨迹掌握复杂交互任务，但当前主流范式受限于浅层的轨迹检索与扁平化的技能总结，从根本上忽视了智能体行为的时间依赖性和以结果为导向的拓扑结构，导致经验复用效率低下。

Method: 提出 Trace2Tower 框架：(1) 将步骤级交互抽象为规范事件，构建由语义兼容性、转换动力学和结果证据共同约束的统一图；(2) 通过新颖的对比谱分解，提取稳定且与成功对齐的行为模式，同时严格抑制易失败的捷径；(3) 这些模式自组织地填充一个动态技能塔，包含动作模板、程序化例程和总体任务策略三个层级；(4) 借助验证器引导的反馈进行持续动态精炼。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 基准上，Trace2Tower 达到 87.31% 的成功率，仅需 10.35 步和 0.26 次无效动作；在 WebShop 基准上达到 50.67% 的精确成功率。两个基准上均显著优于现有基线，在任务掌握程度与上下文高效的经验复用方面表现出色。

Conclusion: Trace2Tower 通过转换感知的谱分解与层级化技能塔构建，有效解决了轨迹深层依赖与结构化技能提炼问题，在任务掌握和上下文高效的体验复用上均显著优于现有方法，展示了面向复杂交互任务的更强智能体能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on execution traces to master complex interactive tasks. However, current paradigms are bottlenecked by shallow trajectory retrieval and flat skill summarization, fundamentally ignoring the temporal dependencies and outcome-conditioned topology of agent behavior. We introduce Trace2Tower, a transition-aware EigenTrace framework that distills raw trajectories into a robust skill hierarchy.
